- [ ] **Step 7: Write the sessions**

Create `lib/data/sessions.ts`:

```ts
import type { SessionRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// Sessions (accounts spec 2.5). The key is the SHA-256 of the cookie's token (lib/auth/session.ts makes both), so the
// store never holds a token. Supabase Auth replaces this table.

export async function createSession(session: {
  key: string;
  accountId: string;
  createdAt: Date;
  expiresAt: Date;
}): Promise<void> {
  const { sessions } = sampleStore();
  // Tidy away sessions that have run out, so the table can't grow for ever.
  for (const [key, row] of sessions) {
    if (Date.parse(row.expiresAt) <= session.createdAt.getTime()) sessions.delete(key);
  }
  sessions.set(session.key, {
    key: session.key,
    accountId: session.accountId,
    createdAt: session.createdAt.toISOString(),
    expiresAt: session.expiresAt.toISOString(),
  });
}

/** The account a session belongs to, while it hasn't run out. */
export async function findSessionAccountId(key: string, now: Date): Promise<string | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().sessions.get(key);
  return row && Date.parse(row.expiresAt) > now.getTime() ? row.accountId : null;
}

/** An account's sessions, oldest first, for the data export (spec 3.3). */
export async function listSessions(accountId: string): Promise<SessionRow[]> {
  return [...sampleStore().sessions.values()]
    .filter((row) => row.accountId === accountId)
    .sort((a, b) => Date.parse(a.createdAt) - Date.parse(b.createdAt))
    .map((row) => ({ ...row }));
}

/** Signs one browser out (spec 2.5): its session goes, and so does its forms' state. */
export async function deleteSession(key: string): Promise<void> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  store.sessions.delete(key);
  for (const [stateKey, row] of store.formStates) {
    if (row.sessionKey === key) store.formStates.delete(stateKey);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the sign-in data**

Create `lib/data/sign-in.ts`:

```ts
import type { SampleEmailRow, SignInAttemptRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// Sign-in attempts, the code count and sample mode's outbox (accounts spec 2.2, 2.3 and 5).

const HOUR_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;

/** The attempt with this key, while it hasn't run out. A copy: change it, then save it. */
export async function getSignInAttempt(key: string, now: Date): Promise<SignInAttemptRow | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().signInAttempts.get(key);
  return row && Date.parse(row.expiresAt) > now.getTime() ? structuredClone(row) : null;
}

/** Saves an attempt, new or changed, and tidies away ones that have run out, with their sample emails. */
export async function saveSignInAttempt(attempt: SignInAttemptRow, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  for (const [key, row] of store.signInAttempts) {
    if (Date.parse(row.expiresAt) > now.getTime()) continue;
    store.signInAttempts.delete(key);
    store.sampleEmails.delete(key);
  }
  store.signInAttempts.set(attempt.key, structuredClone(attempt));
}

/** Ends an attempt: it and its sample email go. */
export async function deleteSignInAttempt(key: string): Promise<void> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  store.signInAttempts.delete(key);
  store.sampleEmails.delete(key);
}

/** How many codes went to `email` in the hour before `now` (spec 5). */
export async function countCodesSent(email: string, now: Date): Promise<number> {
  const since = now.getTime() - HOUR_MS;
  return (sampleStore().codeSends.get(email) ?? []).filter((at) => Date.parse(at) > since).length;
}

/** Notes a code sent to `email`, forgetting sends more than an hour old. */
export async function recordCodeSent(email: string, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const { codeSends } = sampleStore();
  const since = now.getTime() - HOUR_MS;
  const recent = (codeSends.get(email) ?? []).filter((at) => Date.parse(at) > since);
  codeSends.set(email, [...recent, now.toISOString()]);
}

/** Sample mode's outbox (spec 2.3, plan decision 5): keeps the last code "sent" for an attempt. */
export async function saveSampleEmail(email: SampleEmailRow): Promise<void> {
  sampleStore().sampleEmails.set(email.attemptKey, { ...email });
}

export async function getSampleEmail(attemptKey: string): Promise<SampleEmailRow | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().sampleEmails.get(attemptKey);
  return row ? { ...row } : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the form state**

Create `lib/data/form-states.ts`:

```ts
import type { FormStateRow } from "./rows";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// A signed-in form's state after a post (accounts plan decision 3): its errors and typed values, or a notice. One row
// per session and form, so another browser, or another form, never sees it.

const keyFor = (sessionKey: string, form: string) => `${sessionKey}:${form}`;

/** The form's state, while it hasn't run out. A copy. */
export async function getFormState(sessionKey: string, form: string, now: Date): Promise<FormStateRow | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().formStates.get(keyFor(sessionKey, form));
  return row && Date.parse(row.expiresAt) > now.getTime() ? structuredClone(row) : null;
}

/** Saves a form's state, replacing the last one, and tidies away states that have run out. */
export async function saveFormState(state: Omit<FormStateRow, "key">, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const { formStates } = sampleStore();
  for (const [key, row] of formStates) {
    if (Date.parse(row.expiresAt) <= now.getTime()) formStates.delete(key);
  }
  const key = keyFor(state.sessionKey, state.form);
  formStates.set(key, structuredClone({ ...state, key }));
}

export async function clearFormState(sessionKey: string, form: string): Promise<void> {
  sampleStore().formStates.delete(keyFor(sessionKey, form));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Write the accounts**

Create `lib/data/accounts.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { sampleAccounts, sampleConsents } from "@/data/fixtures/accounts";
import type { Account, Consent, SellerStatus, StaffRole, ViewerActivity } from "@/lib/types";
import type { AccountRow } from "./rows";
import { listSessions } from "./sessions";
import { sampleStore } from "./store";

// Accounts, their shops and their consent (accounts spec section 4), shaped like the Supabase calls that will replace
// them: async, by id or by email, handing out copies. Times come from the caller, so nothing here reads the clock.
// Only Server Actions create, change or delete; pages only read.

function toAccount(row: AccountRow): Account {
  const shop = sampleStore().sellerProfiles.get(row.id);
  return {
    id: row.id,
    email: row.email,
    firstName: row.firstName,
    campusSlug: row.campusSlug,
    roles: [...row.roles],
    seller: shop ? { status: shop.status, sellerSlug: shop.sellerSlug } : null,
    createdAt: row.createdAt,
  };
}

export async function getAccount(id: string): Promise<Account | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().accounts.get(id);
  return row ? toAccount(row) : null;
}

/** The account with this (normalised) email, if there is one. */
export async function findAccountByEmail(email: string): Promise<Account | null> {
  for (const row of sampleStore().accounts.values()) {
    if (row.email === email) return toAccount(row);
  }
  return null;
}

/** A new student account (spec 2.6). Its id is random, as Supabase's would be. */
export async function createAccount(details: {
  email: string;
  firstName: string;
  campusSlug: string;
  now: Date;
}): Promise<Account> {
  const row: AccountRow = {
    id: `acc-${randomUUID()}`,
    email: details.email,
    firstName: details.firstName,
    campusSlug: details.campusSlug,
    roles: [],
    createdAt: details.now.toISOString(),
  };
  sampleStore().accounts.set(row.id, row);
  return toAccount(row);
}

/** "Edit details" (spec 3.2). Null when the account has gone. */
export async function updateAccountDetails(
  id: string,
  details: { firstName: string; campusSlug: string },
): Promise<Account | null> {
  const row = sampleStore().accounts.get(id);
  if (!row) return null;
  row.firstName = details.firstName;
  row.campusSlug = details.campusSlug;
  return toAccount(row);
}

/** Records that the account agreed to `version` at `at` (spec 2.7). Once per version: a repeat changes nothing. */
export async function recordConsent(accountId: string, version: string, at: Date): Promise<void> {
  const { consents } = sampleStore();
  for (const row of consents.values()) {
    if (row.accountId === accountId && row.version === version) return;
  }
  const id = `consent-${randomUUID()}`;
  consents.set(id, { id, accountId, version, agreedAt: at.toISOString() });
}

/** The newest consent, for the privacy settings page (spec 3.3). */
export async function getLatestConsent(accountId: string): Promise<Consent | null> {
  let latest: Consent | null = null;
  for (const row of sampleStore().consents.values()) {
    if (row.accountId !== accountId) continue;
    if (!latest || Date.parse(row.agreedAt) > Date.parse(latest.agreedAt)) {
      latest = { version: row.version, agreedAt: row.agreedAt };
    }
  }
  return latest;
}

/**
 * The numbers for the context strip (spec 2.9 and 3.4), or null when the account has gone. Zeros until stage E counts
 * new messages and sales to confirm; saves stay on the device (spec 2.12), so none "come back" through the account.
 */
export async function getAccountActivity(accountId: string): Promise<ViewerActivity | null> {
  if (!sampleStore().accounts.has(accountId)) return null;
  return { newMessages: 0, salesToConfirm: 0, savedItemsBack: 0 };
}

/** The sample accounts' buttons on the sign-in page (spec 2.8), in the fixture's order. */
export async function getSampleSignIns(): Promise<{ email: string; label: string }[]> {
  return sampleAccounts.map(({ account, label }) => ({ email: account.email, label }));
}

/**
 * Puts a deleted sample account back from the fixtures, with its shop and consent (plan decision 15). An account that
 * already has the address, the sample itself or one made after it was deleted, is returned as it is. Null for an
 * address that isn't a sample account's.
 */
export async function restoreSampleAccount(email: string): Promise<Account | null> {
  const sample = sampleAccounts.find(({ account }) => account.email === email);
  if (!sample) return null;
  const existing = await findAccountByEmail(email);
  if (existing) return existing;
  const { account, seller } = structuredClone(sample);
  const store = sampleStore();
  store.accounts.set(account.id, account);
  if (seller) store.sellerProfiles.set(seller.accountId, seller);
  for (const consent of sampleConsents) {
    if (consent.accountId === account.id) store.consents.set(consent.id, { ...consent });
  }
  return toAccount(account);
}

/** "Download your data" (spec 3.3, plan decision 20). Later stages add `messages`, `reports` and `listings`. */
export type AccountExport = {
  exportedAt: string;
  account: {
    email: string;
    firstName: string;
    campus: string;
    roles: StaffRole[];
    memberSince: string;
    shop: { status: SellerStatus; storefront: string | null } | null;
  };
  consents: { policy: string; agreedAt: string }[];
  /** When each session started and ends. Never an id. */
  sessions: { signedInAt: string; expiresAt: string }[];
};

/** The account's data, as the export gives it. Null when the account has gone. */
export async function exportAccountData(accountId: string, now: Date): Promise<AccountExport | null> {
  const account = await getAccount(accountId);
  if (!account) return null;
  const consents = [...sampleStore().consents.values()]
    .filter((row) => row.accountId === accountId)
    .sort((a, b) => Date.parse(a.agreedAt) - Date.parse(b.agreedAt))
    .map((row) => ({ policy: row.version, agreedAt: row.agreedAt }));
  const sessions = (await listSessions(accountId)).map((row) => ({ signedInAt: row.createdAt, expiresAt: row.expiresAt }));
  return {
    exportedAt: now.toISOString(),
    account: {
      email: account.email,
      firstName: account.firstName,
      campus: account.campusSlug,
      roles: [...account.roles],
      memberSince: account.createdAt,
      shop: account.seller ? { status: account.seller.status, storefront: account.seller.sellerSlug } : null,
    },
    consents,
    sessions,
  };
}

/**
 * Deletes an account straight away (spec 2.13): the account, its shop, its consent, its sessions and their forms'
 * state, any sign-in under way for its email, and its code count. Later stages add their tables here (messages,
 * listings, reports). Nothing of anyone else's is touched.
 */
export async function deleteAccountData(accountId: string): Promise<void> {
  const store = sampleStore();
  const account = store.accounts.get(accountId);
  if (!account) return;
  store.accounts.delete(accountId);
  store.sellerProfiles.delete(accountId);
  for (const [id, row] of store.consents) {
    if (row.accountId === accountId) store.consents.delete(id);
  }
  const sessionKeys = new Set<string>();
  for (const [key, row] of store.sessions) {
    if (row.accountId !== accountId) continue;
    sessionKeys.add(key);
    store.sessions.delete(key);
  }
  for (const [key, row] of store.formStates) {
    if (sessionKeys.has(row.sessionKey)) store.formStates.delete(key);
  }
  for (const [key, row] of store.signInAttempts) {
    if (row.email !== account.email) continue;
    store.signInAttempts.delete(key);
    store.sampleEmails.delete(key);
  }
  store.codeSends.delete(account.email);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the stand-ins for Next's request APIs**

Create `tests/helpers/next-request.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Stand-ins for next/headers, next/navigation and next/server in unit tests of server code (accounts plan, Task 4).
 * The request is a plain object made with vi.hoisted, so the vi.mock factories can reach it:
 *
 *   const request = vi.hoisted(
 *     (): FakeRequest => ({ cookies: new Map(), sent: new Map(), proto: "http" }),
 *   );
 *   vi.mock("next/headers", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextHeaders(request));
 *   vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
 *   vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());
 *
 * Call resetRequest(request) in beforeEach.
 */
export type FakeRequest = {
  /** The cookies the browser sends, by name. A write changes them, as the browser would on the response. */
  cookies: Map<string, string>;
  /** Every cookie the response set or deleted, by name, with its options. A delete records maxAge 0. */
  sent: Map<string, { value: string; options: Record<string, unknown> }>;
  /** The x-forwarded-proto header. */
  proto: string;
};

export function resetRequest(request: FakeRequest): void {
  request.cookies.clear();
  request.sent.clear();
  request.proto = "http";
}

export function nextHeaders(request: FakeRequest) {
  return {
    cookies: async () => ({
      get: (name: string) => (request.cookies.has(name) ? { name, value: request.cookies.get(name)! } : undefined),
      set: (name: string, value: string, options: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => {
        request.sent.set(name, { value, options });
        if (options.maxAge === 0) request.cookies.delete(name);
        else request.cookies.set(name, value);
      },
      delete: (name: string) => {
        request.sent.set(name, { value: "", options: { maxAge: 0 } });
        request.cookies.delete(name);
      },
    }),
    headers: async () => ({
      get: (name: string) => (name === "x-forwarded-proto" ? request.proto : null),
    }),
  };
}

/** What the stand-in redirect() throws: where it was going, and how ("push" or "replace"). */
export class RedirectSignal extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly url: string,
    readonly type: string | undefined,
  ) {
    super(`redirect to ${url}`);
    this.name = "RedirectSignal";
  }
}

export function nextNavigation() {
  return {
    redirect: (url: string, type?: string): never => {
      throw new RedirectSignal(url, type);
    },
    notFound: (): never => {
      throw new Error("notFound");
    },
    RedirectType: { push: "push", replace: "replace" },
    usePathname: () => "/",
  };
}

export function nextServer() {
  return { connection: async () => undefined };
}

/** Runs `run` and gives back the redirect it ended with. Fails when it didn't redirect. */
export async function redirectOf(run: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> {
  const signal = await redirectSignalOf(run);
  return signal.url;
}

/** As redirectOf, with the redirect's type too. */
export async function redirectSignalOf(run: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<RedirectSignal> {
  try {
    await run();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.name === "RedirectSignal") return error as RedirectSignal;
    throw error;
  }
  throw new Error("Expected a redirect, but there was none");
}
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createSession } from "@/lib/data/sessions";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "@/lib/data/store";
import { redirectOf, resetRequest, type FakeRequest } from "@/tests/helpers/next-request";
import { SESSION_COOKIE } from "./cookies";
import { hashToken } from "./secrets";
import { endSession, getSessionKey, getViewer, requireAccount, startSession } from "./session";

const request = vi.hoisted((): FakeRequest => ({ cookies: new Map(), sent: new Map(), proto: "http" }));

vi.mock("next/headers", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextHeaders(request));
vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:42:00.000Z");
const IN_30_DAYS = new Date("2026-11-13T08:42:00.000Z");

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
  resetRequest(request);
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
});

/** A stored session for `accountId`, and its token in the browser's cookie. */
async function signedInAs(accountId: string, expiresAt = IN_30_DAYS) {
  await createSession({ key: hashToken("token-1"), accountId, createdAt: NOW, expiresAt });
  request.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, "token-1");
}

describe("getViewer (accounts spec 2.9)", () => {
  it("is null without a session cookie", async () => {
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("is the account whose session the cookie names", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    expect(await getViewer()).toMatchObject({ id: "acc-student", firstName: "Thandi", email: "student@eduvos.com" });
  });

  it("is null once the session has run out, and for a token the store doesn't know", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student", new Date("2026-10-14T08:00:00.000Z"));
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
    request.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, "made-up");
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("trusts nothing but the session: other cookies saying who you are change nothing (spec 2.5)", async () => {
    request.cookies.set("vossie_signed_in", "1");
    request.cookies.set("vossie_account", "acc-admin");
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("is null for the session of an account that has been deleted", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    sampleStore().accounts.delete("acc-student");
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("is null with sample mode off, as there's no backend yet (spec 2.1)", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    vi.stubEnv("VOSSIE_SAMPLE_MODE", "0");
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("requireAccount (accounts spec 2.10)", () => {
  it("gives the signed-in account", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-mentor");
    expect((await requireAccount("/account")).id).toBe("acc-mentor");
  });

  it("sends anyone else to sign in, coming back afterwards", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => requireAccount("/account/privacy"))).toBe("/login?next=%2Faccount%2Fprivacy");
  });
});

describe("starting and ending a session (accounts spec 2.5)", () => {
  it("signs in with a new random token: the browser gets it in an httpOnly cookie, the store only its hash", async () => {
    await startSession("acc-student", NOW);
    const token = request.cookies.get(SESSION_COOKIE)!;
    expect(token).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
    expect(request.sent.get(SESSION_COOKIE)?.options).toEqual({
      httpOnly: true,
      sameSite: "lax",
      secure: false,
      path: "/",
      maxAge: 2_592_000,
    });
    expect(sampleStore().sessions.has(token)).toBe(false);
    expect(sampleStore().sessions.get(hashToken(token))).toMatchObject({
      accountId: "acc-student",
      createdAt: NOW.toISOString(),
      expiresAt: IN_30_DAYS.toISOString(),
    });
    expect(await getSessionKey()).toBe(hashToken(token));
    expect((await getViewer())?.id).toBe("acc-student");
  });

  it("makes the cookie Secure over HTTPS (plan decision 18)", async () => {
    request.proto = "https";
    await startSession("acc-student", NOW);
    expect(request.sent.get(SESSION_COOKIE)?.options.secure).toBe(true);
  });

  it("replaces the browser's old session rather than keeping both", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    await startSession("acc-admin", NOW);
    expect(sampleStore().sessions.has(hashToken("token-1"))).toBe(false);
    expect([...sampleStore().sessions.values()].map((row) => row.accountId)).toEqual(["acc-admin"]);
  });

  it("signs out: the stored session and the cookie both go", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    await endSession();
    expect(sampleStore().sessions.size).toBe(0);
    expect(request.cookies.has(SESSION_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
    expect(request.sent.get(SESSION_COOKIE)?.options.maxAge).toBe(0);
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Create `lib/flash.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * A one-off confirmation after a Server Action redirects (accounts plan decision 12). The cookie holds a code, never
 * words, so it can't put its own text on the page; it's readable, so the toast can delete it once shown.
 */
export const FLASH_COOKIE = "vossie_flash";

const FLASH_MESSAGES = {
  "signed-out": "You're signed out",
  deleted: "Your account has been deleted",
} as const;

export type FlashCode = keyof typeof FLASH_MESSAGES;

/** The words for a flash code, or null for anything else. */
export function flashMessage(code: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  return code && Object.hasOwn(FLASH_MESSAGES, code) ? FLASH_MESSAGES[code as FlashCode] : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the cookie helpers**

Create `lib/auth/cookies.ts`:

```ts
import { cookies, headers } from "next/headers";
import { FLASH_COOKIE, type FlashCode } from "@/lib/flash";
import { FLASH_TTL_SECONDS } from "./config";

// The cookies sign-in uses (accounts spec 2.2 and 2.5, plan decision 12). Reading works anywhere on the server inside
// <Suspense>; setting and deleting only in Server Actions and route handlers.

/** The session: a random token, httpOnly, 30 days (spec 2.5). */
export const SESSION_COOKIE = "vossie_session";
/** A sign-in in progress: a random token, httpOnly, 30 minutes (spec 2.2). */
export const SIGN_IN_COOKIE = "vossie_signin";

/** Whether the request came over HTTPS (plan decision 18). Next sets x-forwarded-proto, and so do hosts. */
export async function isHttps(): Promise<boolean> {
  const proto = (await headers()).get("x-forwarded-proto") ?? "";
  return proto.split(",")[0].trim() === "https";
}

export async function readCookieValue(name: string): Promise<string | null> {
  return (await cookies()).get(name)?.value ?? null;
}

/** A cookie only the server can read: httpOnly, Lax, the whole site, and Secure on HTTPS (spec 2.5). */
export async function setPrivateCookie(name: string, value: string, maxAgeSeconds: number): Promise<void> {
  (await cookies()).set(name, value, {
    httpOnly: true,
    sameSite: "lax",
    secure: await isHttps(),
    path: "/",
    maxAge: maxAgeSeconds,
  });
}

export async function deleteCookie(name: string): Promise<void> {
  (await cookies()).delete(name);
}

/** Leaves a flash code for the next page (plan decision 12): readable, so the toast can delete it once shown. */
export async function setFlash(code: FlashCode): Promise<void> {
  (await cookies()).set(FLASH_COOKIE, code, {
    sameSite: "lax",
    secure: await isHttps(),
    path: "/",
    maxAge: FLASH_TTL_SECONDS,
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the session**

Create `lib/auth/session.ts`:

```ts
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { cache } from "react";
import { getAccount } from "@/lib/data/accounts";
import { createSession, deleteSession, findSessionAccountId } from "@/lib/data/sessions";
import { signInHref } from "@/lib/next-path";
import type { Account } from "@/lib/types";
import { isSampleMode, SESSION_TTL_MS } from "./config";
import { deleteCookie, readCookieValue, SESSION_COOKIE, setPrivateCookie } from "./cookies";
import { hashToken, newToken } from "./secrets";

/** The store's key for this browser's session, or null when it has no session cookie. Request-time. */
export async function getSessionKey(): Promise<string | null> {
  const token = await readCookieValue(SESSION_COOKIE);
  return token ? hashToken(token) : null;
}

/**
 * Who is signed in (accounts spec 2.9): the account, or null. The server trusts only the httpOnly session cookie,
 * looked up by its hash in the store; no other cookie says anything about who you are (plan decision 1).
 *
 * Request-time: call it inside <Suspense>. React's cache() makes it one lookup per render. A Server Action or route
 * handler calls it too; there it simply runs.
 */
export const getViewer = cache(async (): Promise<Account | null> => {
  if (!isSampleMode()) return null;
  const key = await getSessionKey();
  if (!key) return null;
  // The expiry check reads the clock, which Next allows once the render waits for the request (decision 26).
  await connection();
  const accountId = await findSessionAccountId(key, new Date());
  return accountId ? getAccount(accountId) : null;
});

/** The signed-in account, or a redirect to sign-in that comes back to `nextPath` (spec 2.10). Inside <Suspense>. */
export async function requireAccount(nextPath: string): Promise<Account> {
  const account = await getViewer();
  if (!account) redirect(signInHref(nextPath));
  return account;
}

/**
 * Signs this browser in as `accountId` (spec 2.5): a new random token in the cookie, its hash in the store, 30 days.
 * The browser's old session, if any, goes first, so a session id is never reused. Server Actions only.
 */
export async function startSession(accountId: string, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const oldKey = await getSessionKey();
  if (oldKey) await deleteSession(oldKey);
  const token = newToken();
  await createSession({
    key: hashToken(token),
    accountId,
    createdAt: now,
    expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_TTL_MS),
  });
  await setPrivateCookie(SESSION_COOKIE, token, SESSION_TTL_MS / 1000);
}

/** Signs this browser out (spec 2.5): the stored session and the cookie both go. Server Actions only. */
export async function endSession(): Promise<void> {
  const key = await getSessionKey();
  if (key) await deleteSession(key);
  await deleteCookie(SESSION_COOKIE);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Ask the session instead of the switch**

Replace `lib/data/viewer.ts` with:

```ts
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { cache } from "react";
import { getViewer } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { CAMPUS_COOKIE, parseCampusChoice } from "@/lib/campus";
import type { Viewer, ViewerActivity } from "@/lib/types";
import { getAccountActivity } from "./accounts";
import { getCampuses } from "./index";

/** Activity worth a "Welcome back" strip: any number above zero. Otherwise the strip falls back (accounts spec 3.4). */
function worthShowing(activity: ViewerActivity | null): ViewerActivity | null {
  if (!activity) return null;
  return activity.newMessages + activity.salesToConfirm + activity.savedItemsBack > 0 ? activity : null;
}

/**
 * Who is looking (spec section 13): the campus from the vossie_campus cookie and, from the session, whether they're
 * signed in and what the context strip should say (accounts spec 2.9 and 3.4).
 *
 * Request-time: call it only inside <Suspense>, or the page can't be prerendered. React's cache() makes it one read
 * per request.
 */
export const getViewerContext = cache(async (): Promise<Viewer> => {
  // Every section awaits the viewer before its own error handling, so a failed campus or session lookup must not
  // escape here. With no campuses the choice reads as "all campuses"; with no session, signed out.
  const [store, campuses, account] = await Promise.all([
    cookies(),
    getCampuses().catch(() => []),
    getViewer().catch(() => null),
  ]);
  const campus = parseCampusChoice(store.get(CAMPUS_COOKIE)?.value, campuses);
  if (!account) return { campus, signedIn: false, activity: null };
  const activity = await getAccountActivity(account.id).catch(() => null);
  return { campus, signedIn: true, activity: worthShowing(activity) };
});
```

### Task 5: The sign-in Server Actions

Steps 1 to 3, Send a new code, Use a different email, and the sample accounts' buttons (spec 2.2 to 2.8 and 3.1), as Server Actions. The page that posts to them comes in Task 7.

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/auth/actions.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Read the docs**

Read `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/server-actions.md` (the single response, the security section), `02-guides/forms.md` (forms in Server Components, `formData.get`) and `03-api-reference/01-directives/use-server.md`. Note:
- a `"use server"` file may export only async functions;
- every action is a public endpoint, so it checks its own input and takes identity only from the session;
- an action that sets a cookie and redirects gives a 303 without JavaScript, and with it a navigation that renders the destination with the new cookie.

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createAccount, findAccountByEmail, getLatestConsent } from "@/lib/data/accounts";
import { getSampleEmail, recordCodeSent } from "@/lib/data/sign-in";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "@/lib/data/store";
import { redirectOf, redirectSignalOf, resetRequest, type FakeRequest } from "@/tests/helpers/next-request";
import { cancelSignIn, completeProfile, resendCode, signInAsSample, startSignIn, verifyCode } from "./actions";
import { SESSION_COOKIE, SIGN_IN_COOKIE } from "./cookies";
import { hashToken } from "./secrets";
import { getViewer } from "./session";

const request = vi.hoisted((): FakeRequest => ({ cookies: new Map(), sent: new Map(), proto: "http" }));

vi.mock("next/headers", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextHeaders(request));
vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:42:00.000Z");

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
  resetRequest(request);
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
});

/** A form's fields, as the browser posts them. */
function form(fields: Record<string, string>): FormData {
  const data = new FormData();
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(fields)) data.append(name, value);
  return data;
}

/** This browser's attempt, straight from the store, or null. */
function attempt() {
  const token = request.cookies.get(SIGN_IN_COOKIE);
  return token ? (sampleStore().signInAttempts.get(hashToken(token)) ?? null) : null;
}

/** The code sample mode "sent" for this browser's attempt. */
async function sentCode(): Promise<string> {
  return (await getSampleEmail(attempt()!.key))!.code;
}

/** A code that isn't the one sent. */
async function wrongCode(): Promise<string> {
  return (await sentCode()) === "111111" ? "222222" : "111111";
}

/** Step 1 for `email`, with the box ticked, coming back to `next`. */
function start(email: string, next = "/listings/l-001") {
  return redirectSignalOf(() => startSignIn(form({ email, consent: "yes", next })));
}

describe("step 1: the email and the privacy box (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("sends a code to an Eduvos email and moves on to step 2, keeping next in the address", async () => {
    const redirect = await start("  Thandi@Eduvos.com ");
    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type]).toEqual(["/login?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001", "replace"]);
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({
      step: "code",
      email: "thandi@eduvos.com",
      errors: [],
      wrongTries: 0,
      consentedAt: NOW.toISOString(),
      codeExpiresAt: "2026-10-14T08:52:00.000Z",
    });
    expect(await sentCode()).toMatch(/^\d{6}$/);
  });

  it("keeps the attempt behind an httpOnly cookie for 30 minutes, with only its token's hash in the store", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    const token = request.cookies.get(SIGN_IN_COOKIE)!;
    expect(request.sent.get(SIGN_IN_COOKIE)?.options).toMatchObject({
      httpOnly: true,
      sameSite: "lax",
      path: "/",
      maxAge: 1800,
    });
    expect(sampleStore().signInAttempts.has(token)).toBe(false);
    expect(attempt()?.expiresAt).toBe("2026-10-14T09:12:00.000Z");
  });

  it("keeps only the code's hash on the attempt (spec 5)", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    const code = await sentCode();
    expect(attempt()?.codeHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
    expect(JSON.stringify(attempt())).not.toContain(code);
  });

  it("asks for the email and the box together", async () => {
    await redirectSignalOf(() => startSignIn(form({ email: " ", next: "/listings/l-001" })));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "email", errors: ["email-missing", "consent-missing"], consentedAt: null });
    expect(sampleStore().sampleEmails.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("turns other domains away, keeping what was typed so the field can show it", async () => {
    await start("thandi@gmail.com");
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "email", email: "thandi@gmail.com", errors: ["email-domain"] });
    expect(sampleStore().sampleEmails.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("allows 5 codes an hour for an email (spec 5)", async () => {
    for (const minutes of [0, 5, 10, 15, 20]) {
      await recordCodeSent("thandi@eduvos.com", new Date(NOW.getTime() - minutes * 60_000));
    }
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "email", errors: ["email-rate"] });
  });

  it("starts a new attempt under a new token each time", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    const firstToken = request.cookies.get(SIGN_IN_COOKIE);
    const firstKey = attempt()!.key;
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    expect(request.cookies.get(SIGN_IN_COOKIE)).not.toBe(firstToken);
    expect(sampleStore().signInAttempts.has(firstKey)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("drops a next that leads off the site", async () => {
    expect((await start("thandi@eduvos.com", "//evil.example")).url).toBe("/login");
  });

  it("does nothing with sample mode off (spec 2.1)", async () => {
    vi.stubEnv("VOSSIE_SAMPLE_MODE", "0");
    expect((await start("thandi@eduvos.com")).url).toBe("/login?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001");
    expect(attempt()).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("step 2: the code (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("signs an existing account straight in and follows next", async () => {
    await start("student@eduvos.com");
    const code = await sentCode();
    const redirect = await redirectSignalOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code, next: "/listings/l-001" })));
    // A plain redirect, so it pushes: Back returns to the sign-in page, which now says you're signed in.
    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type]).toEqual(["/listings/l-001", undefined]);
    expect((await getViewer())?.email).toBe("student@eduvos.com");
    expect(request.cookies.has(SIGN_IN_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
    expect(sampleStore().signInAttempts.size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("goes to the account page when next is missing, or is the sign-in page itself (plan decision 9)", async () => {
    await redirectSignalOf(() => startSignIn(form({ email: "student@eduvos.com", consent: "yes" })));
    expect(await redirectOf(async () => verifyCode(form({ code: await sentCode() })))).toBe("/account");
    await start("student@eduvos.com", "/login?next=%2Fsaved");
    expect(await redirectOf(async () => verifyCode(form({ code: await sentCode(), next: "/login?next=%2Fsaved" })))).toBe(
      "/account",
    );
  });

  it("ignores spaces in the code", async () => {
    await start("student@eduvos.com");
    const code = await sentCode();
    const spaced = `${code.slice(0, 3)} ${code.slice(3)}`;
    expect(await redirectOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code: spaced, next: "/saved" })))).toBe("/saved");
  });

  it("moves a new email on to step 3 without making an account yet (plan decision 8)", async () => {
    await start("new@eduvos.com");
    const code = await sentCode();
    expect(await redirectOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code, next: "/listings/l-001" })))).toBe(
      "/login?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001",
    );
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "profile", codeHash: null, errors: [] });
    expect(await findAccountByEmail("new@eduvos.com")).toBeNull();
    expect(request.cookies.has(SESSION_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("counts wrong tries, and the fifth ends the attempt, back on step 1 with the email kept", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    const wrong = await wrongCode();
    for (let tries = 1; tries <= 4; tries++) {
      await redirectSignalOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code: wrong })));
      expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "code", wrongTries: tries, errors: ["code-wrong"] });
    }
    await redirectSignalOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code: wrong })));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "email", email: "thandi@eduvos.com", codeHash: null, errors: ["locked"] });
  });

  it("refuses the right code once its 10 minutes are up", async () => {
    await start("student@eduvos.com");
    const code = await sentCode();
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-10-14T08:52:00.000Z"));
    await redirectSignalOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code })));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "code", errors: ["code-expired"] });
    expect(request.cookies.has(SESSION_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("asks for 6 digits without using a try (plan decision 6)", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    for (const code of ["", "12345", "1234567", "12a456"]) {
      await redirectSignalOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code })));
      expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ errors: ["code-missing"], wrongTries: 0 });
    }
  });

  it("sends a browser with no attempt back to step 1", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code: "123456", next: "/saved" })))).toBe("/login?next=%2Fsaved");
    expect(request.cookies.has(SESSION_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("sending a new code (plan decision 6)", () => {
  it("sends a new code with 5 fresh tries, and says so", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    await redirectSignalOf(async () => verifyCode(form({ code: await wrongCode() })));
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-10-14T08:43:00.000Z"));
    await redirectSignalOf(() => resendCode(form({})));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({
      step: "code",
      wrongTries: 0,
      errors: [],
      resent: true,
      codeExpiresAt: "2026-10-14T08:53:00.000Z",
    });
    expect(sampleStore().codeSends.get("thandi@eduvos.com")).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("stops at 5 codes an hour", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    for (let sent = 2; sent <= 5; sent++) await redirectSignalOf(() => resendCode(form({})));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ resent: true, errors: [] });
    await redirectSignalOf(() => resendCode(form({})));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ step: "code", resent: false, errors: ["code-rate"] });
  });

  it("stops saying \"We sent a new code\" after the next try", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    await redirectSignalOf(() => resendCode(form({})));
    await redirectSignalOf(async () => verifyCode(form({ code: await wrongCode() })));
    expect(attempt()?.resent).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("using a different email (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("ends the attempt and goes back to an empty step 1", async () => {
    await start("thandi@eduvos.com");
    const key = attempt()!.key;
    expect(await redirectOf(() => cancelSignIn(form({ next: "/listings/l-001" })))).toBe("/login?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001");
    expect(request.cookies.has(SIGN_IN_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
    expect(sampleStore().signInAttempts.has(key)).toBe(false);
    expect(await getSampleEmail(key)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("step 3: first sign-in (accounts spec 2.6)", () => {
  async function toStep3() {
    await start("new@eduvos.com");
    const code = await sentCode();
    await redirectSignalOf(() => verifyCode(form({ code })));
  }

  it("makes the account with the name and campus, records consent, signs in and follows next", async () => {
    await toStep3();
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-10-14T08:45:00.000Z"));
    const done = await redirectOf(() =>
      completeProfile(form({ firstName: "  Ayanda ", campus: "pretoria", next: "/listings/l-001" })),
    );
    expect(done).toBe("/listings/l-001");
    const account = await findAccountByEmail("new@eduvos.com");
    expect(account).toMatchObject({ firstName: "Ayanda", campusSlug: "pretoria", roles: [], createdAt: "2026-10-14T08:45:00.000Z" });
    // Consent dates from when the box was ticked, on step 1 (spec 2.7).
    expect(await getLatestConsent(account!.id)).toEqual({ version: "2026-10-draft", agreedAt: NOW.toISOString() });
    expect((await getViewer())?.id).toBe(account!.id);
    expect(request.cookies.has(SIGN_IN_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("keeps step 3's answers and says what's wrong, making nothing", async () => {
    await toStep3();
    await redirectSignalOf(() => completeProfile(form({ firstName: "A", campus: "atlantis" })));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({
      step: "profile",
      errors: ["first-name-length", "campus-missing"],
      firstName: "A",
      campusSlug: "",
    });
    await redirectSignalOf(() => completeProfile(form({ firstName: "", campus: "midrand" })));
    expect(attempt()).toMatchObject({ errors: ["first-name-missing"], campusSlug: "midrand" });
    expect(await findAccountByEmail("new@eduvos.com")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("signs in to the account another tab made first, rather than making a second", async () => {
    await toStep3();
    const other = await createAccount({ email: "new@eduvos.com", firstName: "Zola", campusSlug: "midrand", now: NOW });
    await redirectSignalOf(() => completeProfile(form({ firstName: "Ayanda", campus: "pretoria" })));
    expect((await getViewer())?.id).toBe(other.id);
    expect([...sampleStore().accounts.values()].filter((row) => row.email === "new@eduvos.com")).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("can't be reached without the right code", async () => {
    await start("new@eduvos.com");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => completeProfile(form({ firstName: "Ayanda", campus: "pretoria" })))).toBe("/login");
    expect(await findAccountByEmail("new@eduvos.com")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("can't be finished once the attempt's 30 minutes are up", async () => {
    await toStep3();
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-10-14T09:12:00.000Z"));
    expect(await redirectOf(() => completeProfile(form({ firstName: "Ayanda", campus: "pretoria" })))).toBe("/login");
    expect(await findAccountByEmail("new@eduvos.com")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the actions**

Create `lib/auth/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";

import { redirect, RedirectType } from "next/navigation";
import { getCampuses } from "@/lib/data";
import { createAccount, findAccountByEmail, recordConsent, restoreSampleAccount } from "@/lib/data/accounts";
import type { SignInAttemptRow, SignInError } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import {
  countCodesSent,
  deleteSignInAttempt,
  getSignInAttempt,
  recordCodeSent,
  saveSampleEmail,
  saveSignInAttempt,
} from "@/lib/data/sign-in";
import { safeNextPath, signInHref } from "@/lib/next-path";
import { ATTEMPT_TTL_MS, CODE_TTL_MS, CODES_PER_HOUR, isSampleMode, MAX_WRONG_TRIES, POLICY_VERSION } from "./config";
import { deleteCookie, readCookieValue, SIGN_IN_COOKIE, setPrivateCookie } from "./cookies";
import { checkEmail, normaliseEmail } from "./email";
import { checkCampus, checkFirstName } from "./profile";
import { codeMatches, hashCode, hashToken, newCode, newToken } from "./secrets";
import { startSession } from "./session";

// The Server Actions behind every account form (accounts spec 2 and 3). Each is a plain form's action, so it works
// before the app's JavaScript arrives (plan decision 2). It checks its own input, takes identity only from the
// session, keeps errors in the store rather than the URL (plan decision 3), and ends with a redirect: back to the same
// page with `replace` while a form is under way (plan decision 19), elsewhere once it's done.

// --- Shared helpers ---------------------------------------------------------

/** A form field as text. A missing field, or a file, is "". */
function text(formData: FormData, name: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(name);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : "";
}

/** The `next` path the form carried, when it's a safe path on this site (spec 2.10). */
function nextFrom(formData: FormData): string | null {
  return safeNextPath(text(formData, "next") || undefined);
}

/** The sign-in page again, keeping `next`. */
function backToSignIn(next: string | null): never {
  redirect(next ? signInHref(next) : "/login", RedirectType.replace);
}

/** Where a finished sign-in goes (plan decision 9): `next`, unless it's the sign-in page itself; else the account. */
function afterSignIn(next: string | null): never {
  redirect(next && !next.startsWith("/login") ? next : "/account");
}

// --- Signing in ---------------------------------------------------------------

/** This browser's sign-in attempt, while it hasn't run out. */
async function currentAttempt(now: Date): Promise<SignInAttemptRow | null> {
  const token = await readCookieValue(SIGN_IN_COOKIE);
  return token ? getSignInAttempt(hashToken(token), now) : null;
}

/** Ends this browser's attempt, if it has one, and clears its cookie. */
async function endAttempt(now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const attempt = await currentAttempt(now);
  if (attempt) await deleteSignInAttempt(attempt.key);
  await deleteCookie(SIGN_IN_COOKIE);
}

/**
 * A new attempt under a new token, replacing this browser's old one, so a token is never reused. It has 30 minutes
 * for all three steps. The caller saves it.
 */
async function beginAttempt(
  now: Date,
  fields: Pick<SignInAttemptRow, "email" | "errors" | "consentedAt">,
): Promise<SignInAttemptRow> {
  const old = await currentAttempt(now);
  if (old) await deleteSignInAttempt(old.key);
  const token = newToken();
  await setPrivateCookie(SIGN_IN_COOKIE, token, ATTEMPT_TTL_MS / 1000);
  return {
    key: hashToken(token),
    step: "email",
    resent: false,
    codeHash: null,
    codeExpiresAt: null,
    wrongTries: 0,
    firstName: "",
    campusSlug: "",
    expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + ATTEMPT_TTL_MS).toISOString(),
    ...fields,
  };
}

/**
 * Makes a code, keeps only its hash on the attempt, and "sends" it (spec 2.2, 2.3 and 5). A new code gives 5 fresh
 * tries (plan decision 6). False, and nothing sent, when this email has had its 5 codes this hour.
 */
async function sendCode(attempt: SignInAttemptRow, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
  if ((await countCodesSent(attempt.email, now)) >= CODES_PER_HOUR) return false;
  const code = newCode();
  attempt.codeHash = hashCode(attempt.key, code);
  attempt.codeExpiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + CODE_TTL_MS).toISOString();
  attempt.wrongTries = 0;
  await recordCodeSent(attempt.email, now);
  // Sample mode has no email service: its outbox stands in for one, and the sign-in page shows the code from it
  // (plan decision 5). The real email service replaces this call.
  await saveSampleEmail({ attemptKey: attempt.key, to: attempt.email, code, sentAt: now.toISOString() });
  return true;
}

/** Finishes signing in: consent recorded, the attempt gone, a new session (spec 2.5 and 2.7). */
async function finishSignIn(accountId: string, attempt: SignInAttemptRow, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  await recordConsent(accountId, POLICY_VERSION, attempt.consentedAt ? new Date(attempt.consentedAt) : now);
  await deleteSignInAttempt(attempt.key);
  await deleteCookie(SIGN_IN_COOKIE);
  await startSession(accountId, now);
}

/**
 * Step 1 (spec 3.1): an Eduvos email and the privacy policy box. A good email gets a code and moves on to step 2;
 * anything wrong stays on step 1, with the email as typed.
 */
export async function startSignIn(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const next = nextFrom(formData);
  if (!isSampleMode()) backToSignIn(next);
  const now = new Date();
  const email = normaliseEmail(text(formData, "email"));
  const consented = text(formData, "consent") === "yes";
  const errors: SignInError[] = [];
  const check = checkEmail(email);
  if (check === "missing") errors.push("email-missing");
  if (check === "domain") errors.push("email-domain");
  if (!consented) errors.push("consent-missing");
  const attempt = await beginAttempt(now, {
    email: email.slice(0, 254),
    errors,
    consentedAt: consented ? now.toISOString() : null,
  });
  if (errors.length === 0) {
    if (await sendCode(attempt, now)) attempt.step = "code";
    else attempt.errors = ["email-rate"];
  }
  await saveSignInAttempt(attempt, now);
  backToSignIn(next);
}

/**
 * Step 2 (spec 3.1): the 6-digit code. Right, for an existing account: signed in. Right, for a new email: on to
 * step 3. Wrong: a try used, and the fifth ends the attempt, back on step 1 (spec 2.2). Expired: say so. Not 6 digits:
 * say so, without using a try.
 */
export async function verifyCode(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const next = nextFrom(formData);
  const now = new Date();
  const attempt = isSampleMode() ? await currentAttempt(now) : null;
  if (!attempt || attempt.step !== "code" || !attempt.codeHash || !attempt.codeExpiresAt) backToSignIn(next);
  const code = text(formData, "code").replace(/\s/g, "");
  attempt.resent = false;
  if (Date.parse(attempt.codeExpiresAt) <= now.getTime()) {
    attempt.errors = ["code-expired"];
  } else if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(code)) {
    attempt.errors = ["code-missing"];
  } else if (codeMatches(attempt.key, code, attempt.codeHash)) {
    const account = await findAccountByEmail(attempt.email);
    if (account) {
      await finishSignIn(account.id, attempt, now);
      afterSignIn(next);
    }
    attempt.step = "profile";
    attempt.codeHash = null;
    attempt.codeExpiresAt = null;
    attempt.errors = [];
  } else {
    attempt.wrongTries += 1;
    if (attempt.wrongTries < MAX_WRONG_TRIES) {
      attempt.errors = ["code-wrong"];
    } else {
      // The fifth wrong try ends the attempt (spec 2.2): back to step 1, the email kept, the code gone.
      attempt.step = "email";
      attempt.codeHash = null;
      attempt.codeExpiresAt = null;
      attempt.errors = ["locked"];
    }
  }
  await saveSignInAttempt(attempt, now);
  backToSignIn(next);
}

/** "Send a new code" on step 2 (plan decision 6): a new code and 5 fresh tries, within the 5 an hour. */
export async function resendCode(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const next = nextFrom(formData);
  const now = new Date();
  const attempt = isSampleMode() ? await currentAttempt(now) : null;
  if (!attempt || attempt.step !== "code") backToSignIn(next);
  const sent = await sendCode(attempt, now);
  attempt.resent = sent;
  attempt.errors = sent ? [] : ["code-rate"];
  await saveSignInAttempt(attempt, now);
  backToSignIn(next);
}

/** "Use a different email" (spec 3.1): the attempt ends, and step 1 starts empty. */
export async function cancelSignIn(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const next = nextFrom(formData);
  await endAttempt(new Date());
  backToSignIn(next);
}

/**
 * Step 3, a first sign-in only (spec 2.6): a first name and a campus. Only now is the account made, consent recorded
 * and the session started (plan decision 8).
 */
export async function completeProfile(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const next = nextFrom(formData);
  const now = new Date();
  const attempt = isSampleMode() ? await currentAttempt(now) : null;
  if (!attempt || attempt.step !== "profile") backToSignIn(next);
  const name = checkFirstName(text(formData, "firstName"));
  const campuses = await getCampuses();
  const campusSlug = checkCampus(text(formData, "campus"), campuses.map((campus) => campus.slug));
  const errors: SignInError[] = [];
  if (name.error) errors.push(name.error);
  if (!campusSlug) errors.push("campus-missing");
  if (errors.length > 0 || !campusSlug) {
    attempt.errors = errors;
    attempt.firstName = name.value.slice(0, 80);
    attempt.campusSlug = campusSlug ?? "";
    await saveSignInAttempt(attempt, now);
    backToSignIn(next);
  }
  // Another tab may have finished first: then this signs in to that account rather than making a second one.
  const account =
    (await findAccountByEmail(attempt.email)) ??
    (await createAccount({ email: attempt.email, firstName: name.value, campusSlug, now }));
  await finishSignIn(account.id, attempt, now);
  afterSignIn(next);
}

/** "Sign in as a sample account" (spec 2.8): straight in, in sample mode only, and only as one of the five. */
export async function signInAsSample(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const next = nextFrom(formData);
  if (!isSampleMode()) backToSignIn(next);
  const account = await restoreSampleAccount(normaliseEmail(text(formData, "email")));
  if (!account) backToSignIn(next);
  const now = new Date();
  await endAttempt(now);
  await startSession(account.id, now);
  afterSignIn(next);
}
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { getAccount } from "@/lib/data/accounts";
import { createSession } from "@/lib/data/sessions";
import { resetSampleStore, sampleStore } from "@/lib/data/store";
import { FLASH_COOKIE } from "@/lib/flash";
import { redirectOf, redirectSignalOf, resetRequest, type FakeRequest } from "@/tests/helpers/next-request";
import { deleteAccount, signOut, updateDetails } from "./actions";
import { SESSION_COOKIE } from "./cookies";
import { DELETE_FORM, DETAILS_FORM, readFormState } from "./form-state";
import { hashToken } from "./secrets";
import { getViewer } from "./session";

const request = vi.hoisted((): FakeRequest => ({ cookies: new Map(), sent: new Map(), proto: "http" }));

vi.mock("next/headers", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextHeaders(request));
vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:42:00.000Z");
const IN_30_DAYS = new Date("2026-11-13T08:42:00.000Z");

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
  resetRequest(request);
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
});

function form(fields: Record<string, string>): FormData {
  const data = new FormData();
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(fields)) data.append(name, value);
  return data;
}

/** A stored session for `accountId`, and its token in the browser's cookie. */
async function signedInAs(accountId: string, token = "token-1") {
  await createSession({ key: hashToken(token), accountId, createdAt: NOW, expiresAt: IN_30_DAYS });
  request.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, token);
}

describe("signing out (accounts spec 2.5)", () => {
  it("ends the session, leaves the toast's code, and goes to the homepage", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    const redirect = await redirectSignalOf(() => signOut());
    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type]).toEqual(["/", undefined]);
    expect(sampleStore().sessions.size).toBe(0);
    expect(request.cookies.has(SESSION_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
    expect(request.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE)).toBe("signed-out");
    expect(request.sent.get(FLASH_COOKIE)?.options).toEqual({ sameSite: "lax", secure: false, path: "/", maxAge: 60 });
  });

  it("does no harm when already signed out", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => signOut())).toBe("/");
  });
});

describe("editing details (accounts spec 3.2)", () => {
  it("saves the first name and campus, and says so", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    const redirect = await redirectSignalOf(() => updateDetails(form({ firstName: " Thandiwe ", campus: "pretoria" })));
    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type]).toEqual(["/account", "replace"]);
    expect(await getAccount("acc-student")).toMatchObject({ firstName: "Thandiwe", campusSlug: "pretoria" });
    expect(await readFormState(DETAILS_FORM)).toMatchObject({ errors: [], notice: "saved" });
  });

  it("keeps what was typed and says what's wrong, changing nothing", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    await redirectSignalOf(() => updateDetails(form({ firstName: "T", campus: "atlantis" })));
    expect(await getAccount("acc-student")).toMatchObject({ firstName: "Thandi", campusSlug: "midrand" });
    expect(await readFormState(DETAILS_FORM)).toMatchObject({
      errors: ["first-name-length", "campus-missing"],
      values: { firstName: "T", campus: "" },
      notice: null,
    });
  });

  it("only ever changes the signed-in account, whatever the form carries (spec 5)", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    await redirectSignalOf(() =>
      updateDetails(form({ firstName: "Mallory", campus: "pretoria", accountId: "acc-admin", email: "admin@eduvos.com" })),
    );
    expect((await getAccount("acc-admin"))?.firstName).toBe("Pieter");
    expect((await getAccount("acc-student"))?.firstName).toBe("Mallory");
  });

  it("sends a signed-out visitor to sign in", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => updateDetails(form({ firstName: "Thandi", campus: "midrand" })))).toBe(
      "/login?next=%2Faccount",
    );
  });
});

describe("deleting the account (accounts spec 2.13)", () => {
  it("asks for the box first, deleting nothing", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-pending");
    const redirect = await redirectSignalOf(() => deleteAccount(form({})));
    expect([redirect.url, redirect.type]).toEqual(["/account/privacy", "replace"]);
    expect(await readFormState(DELETE_FORM)).toMatchObject({ errors: ["confirm-missing"] });
    expect(await getAccount("acc-pending")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("deletes the account and everything with it, signs out, and goes home with a toast", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-pending");
    expect(await redirectOf(() => deleteAccount(form({ confirm: "yes" })))).toBe("/");
    expect(await getAccount("acc-pending")).toBeNull();
    expect(sampleStore().sellerProfiles.has("acc-pending")).toBe(false);
    expect(sampleStore().sessions.size).toBe(0);
    expect(request.cookies.has(SESSION_COOKIE)).toBe(false);
    expect(request.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE)).toBe("deleted");
    expect(await getViewer()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("sends a signed-out visitor to sign in, deleting nothing", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => deleteAccount(form({ confirm: "yes" })))).toBe("/login?next=%2Faccount%2Fprivacy");
    expect(sampleStore().accounts.size).toBe(5);
  });
});

describe("form state (plan decision 3)", () => {
  it("belongs to one session: another browser signed in to the same account doesn't see it", async () => {
    await signedInAs("acc-student");
    await redirectSignalOf(() => updateDetails(form({ firstName: "T", campus: "midrand" })));
    await signedInAs("acc-student", "token-2");
    expect(await readFormState(DETAILS_FORM)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("is none when signed out", async () => {
    expect(await readFormState(DETAILS_FORM)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the form state helpers**

Create `lib/auth/form-state.ts`:

```ts
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { getFormState, saveFormState } from "@/lib/data/form-states";
import type { FormStateRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { FORM_STATE_TTL_MS } from "./config";
import { getSessionKey } from "./session";

// A signed-in form's state after a post (accounts plan decision 3): its errors and typed values, or a notice such as
// "saved". It belongs to this browser's session and lasts 10 minutes. Stages E to G use it for their forms too.

/** The account page's "Edit details" form, and its fields' ids. */
export const DETAILS_FORM = "details";
export const DETAILS_FIELDS = { firstName: "details-first-name", campus: "details-campus" } as const;

/** The privacy settings page's "Delete your account" form, and its field's id. */
export const DELETE_FORM = "delete-account";
export const DELETE_FIELDS = { confirm: "delete-confirm" } as const;

/** This session's state for `form`, or null. Request-time: call it inside <Suspense>. */
export async function readFormState(form: string): Promise<FormStateRow | null> {
  const sessionKey = await getSessionKey();
  if (!sessionKey) return null;
  // The expiry check reads the clock (decision 26).
  await connection();
  return getFormState(sessionKey, form, new Date());
}

/** Keeps `form`'s state for this session, replacing the last. Server Actions only; signed out, it does nothing. */
export async function writeFormState(
  form: string,
  state: Pick<FormStateRow, "errors" | "values" | "notice">,
  now: Date,
): Promise<void> {
  const sessionKey = await getSessionKey();
  if (!sessionKey) return;
  const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + FORM_STATE_TTL_MS).toISOString();
  await saveFormState({ sessionKey, form, ...state, expiresAt }, now);
}
```

Then append to the end of the file:

```ts

// --- Your account ---------------------------------------------------------------

/** Sign out (spec 2.5): the session goes, and the homepage says so in a toast (plan decision 12). */
export async function signOut(): Promise<void> {
  await endSession();
  await setFlash("signed-out");
  redirect("/");
}

/** "Edit details" on the account page (spec 3.2): the first name and the campus, checked as step 3 checks them. */
export async function updateDetails(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const account = await requireAccount("/account");
  const now = new Date();
  const name = checkFirstName(text(formData, "firstName"));
  const campuses = await getCampuses();
  const campusSlug = checkCampus(text(formData, "campus"), campuses.map((campus) => campus.slug));
  const errors: string[] = [];
  if (name.error) errors.push(name.error);
  if (!campusSlug) errors.push("campus-missing");
  if (errors.length > 0 || !campusSlug) {
    const values = { firstName: name.value.slice(0, 80), campus: campusSlug ?? "" };
    await writeFormState(DETAILS_FORM, { errors, values, notice: null }, now);
  } else {
    await updateAccountDetails(account.id, { firstName: name.value, campusSlug });
    // "Your details are saved." is a line on the page, not a toast, so the page needs no client code (plan decision 13).
    await writeFormState(DETAILS_FORM, { errors: [], values: {}, notice: "saved" }, now);
  }
  redirect("/account", RedirectType.replace);
}

/**
 * "Delete my account" (spec 2.13 and 3.3), only with the box ticked. Then the account and everything that hangs off it
 * go at once, this browser is signed out, and the homepage says so in a toast.
 */
export async function deleteAccount(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const account = await requireAccount("/account/privacy");
  const now = new Date();
  if (text(formData, "confirm") !== "yes") {
    await writeFormState(DELETE_FORM, { errors: ["confirm-missing"], values: {}, notice: null }, now);
    redirect("/account/privacy", RedirectType.replace);
  }
  await deleteAccountData(account.id);
  await deleteCookie(SESSION_COOKIE);
  await setFlash("deleted");
  redirect("/");
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { renderToString } from "react-dom/server";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { campuses } from "@/data/fixtures/campuses";
import { CampusSelect } from "./CampusSelect";
import { ErrorSummary } from "./ErrorSummary";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "./FieldError";

const errors = [
  { message: "Use your Eduvos email address.", fieldId: "signin-email" },
  { message: "Too many tries. Start again.", fieldId: null },
];

describe("ErrorSummary (accounts spec 6)", () => {
  it("is nothing when nothing is wrong", () => {
    const { container } = render(<ErrorSummary errors={[]} />);
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
  });

  it("is an alert headed \"There's a problem\" that links each message to its field", () => {
    render(<ErrorSummary errors={errors} />);
    const summary = screen.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" });
    expect(within(summary).getByRole("link", { name: "Use your Eduvos email address." })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "#signin-email",
    );
    expect(within(summary).getByText("Too many tries. Start again.").tagName).toBe("LI");
    expect(summary).toHaveAttribute("tabindex", "-1");
  });

  it("asks the browser to focus it on a page load (plan decision 11)", () => {
    expect(renderToString(<ErrorSummary errors={errors} />)).toMatch(/<div[^>]*role="alert"[^>]*autofocus=""/);
  });
});

describe("FieldError (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("says it's an error, in words", () => {
    render(<FieldError id="signin-email-error" message="Use your Eduvos email address." />);
    expect(document.getElementById("signin-email-error")).toHaveTextContent("Error: Use your Eduvos email address.");
  });

  it("is nothing without a message, and describedBy joins only the ids it's given", () => {
    const { container } = render(<FieldError id="unused" message={undefined} />);
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
    expect(describedBy("hint", undefined, "", "error")).toBe("hint error");
    expect(describedBy(undefined, "")).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("CampusSelect (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("lists every campus after \"Choose your campus\", starting on the one given", () => {
    render(
      <>
        <label htmlFor="campus">Your campus</label>
        <CampusSelect id="campus" defaultValue="pretoria" campuses={campuses} invalid={false} describedBy={undefined} />
      </>,
    );
    const select = screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Your campus" });
    expect(select).toHaveAttribute("name", "campus");
    expect(select).toHaveValue("pretoria");
    expect(within(select).getAllByRole("option").map((option) => option.textContent)).toEqual([
      "Choose your campus",
      ...campuses.map((campus) => campus.name),
    ]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { SignInAttemptRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { createSession } from "@/lib/data/sessions";
import { saveSampleEmail, saveSignInAttempt } from "@/lib/data/sign-in";
import { resetSampleStore } from "@/lib/data/store";
import { makeSignInAttempt } from "@/tests/helpers/fakes";
import { resetRequest, type FakeRequest } from "@/tests/helpers/next-request";
import { SESSION_COOKIE, SIGN_IN_COOKIE } from "./cookies";
import { hashToken } from "./secrets";
import { getSignInView } from "./sign-in-view";

const request = vi.hoisted((): FakeRequest => ({ cookies: new Map(), sent: new Map(), proto: "http" }));

vi.mock("next/headers", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextHeaders(request));
vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:42:00.000Z");

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
  resetRequest(request);
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
});

/** An attempt in the store, and its token in the browser's cookie. */
async function withAttempt(fields: Partial<SignInAttemptRow>) {
  await saveSignInAttempt(makeSignInAttempt({ key: hashToken("attempt-token"), ...fields }), NOW);
  request.cookies.set(SIGN_IN_COOKIE, "attempt-token");
}

describe("getSignInView (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("starts on an empty step 1", async () => {
    expect(await getSignInView()).toEqual({ step: "email", email: "", consented: false, errors: [] });
  });

  it("shows step 1 again with what was typed and what went wrong", async () => {
    await withAttempt({ step: "email", email: "thandi@gmail.com", consentedAt: NOW.toISOString(), errors: ["email-domain"] });
    expect(await getSignInView()).toEqual({
      step: "email",
      email: "thandi@gmail.com",
      consented: true,
      errors: [{ message: "Use your Eduvos email address.", fieldId: "signin-email" }],
    });
  });

  it("shows step 2 with the masked email, the sample code and the tries left", async () => {
    await withAttempt({ step: "code", email: "thandi@eduvos.com", wrongTries: 2, errors: ["code-wrong"] });
    await saveSampleEmail({
      attemptKey: hashToken("attempt-token"),
      to: "thandi@eduvos.com",
      code: "042137",
      sentAt: NOW.toISOString(),
    });
    expect(await getSignInView()).toEqual({
      step: "code",
      maskedEmail: "t•••@eduvos.com",
      errors: [{ message: "That code isn't right. 3 tries left.", fieldId: "signin-code" }],
      resent: false,
      sampleCode: "042137",
    });
  });

  it("starts step 3's campus on the one chosen on this device, or on none (spec 2.6)", async () => {
    await withAttempt({ step: "profile" });
    request.cookies.set("vossie_campus", "pretoria");
    expect(await getSignInView()).toMatchObject({ step: "profile", campus: "pretoria", firstName: "" });
    request.cookies.set("vossie_campus", "all");
    expect(await getSignInView()).toMatchObject({ campus: "" });
  });

  it("keeps step 3's typed answers ahead of the device's campus", async () => {
    await withAttempt({ step: "profile", firstName: "A", campusSlug: "midrand", errors: ["first-name-length"] });
    request.cookies.set("vossie_campus", "pretoria");
    expect(await getSignInView()).toMatchObject({ firstName: "A", campus: "midrand" });
  });

  it("forgets an attempt that has run out", async () => {
    await withAttempt({ step: "code", email: "thandi@eduvos.com", expiresAt: "2026-10-14T08:00:00.000Z" });
    expect((await getSignInView()).step).toBe("email");
  });

  it("says who's signed in, ahead of any attempt", async () => {
    await withAttempt({ step: "code", email: "thandi@eduvos.com" });
    await createSession({
      key: hashToken("session-token"),
      accountId: "acc-student",
      createdAt: NOW,
      expiresAt: new Date("2026-11-13T08:42:00.000Z"),
    });
    request.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, "session-token");
    expect(await getSignInView()).toEqual({ step: "signed-in", email: "student@eduvos.com" });
  });

  it("is unavailable with sample mode off (spec 2.1)", async () => {
    vi.stubEnv("VOSSIE_SAMPLE_MODE", "0");
    expect(await getSignInView()).toEqual({ step: "unavailable" });
  });
});
```
