```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { renderToString } from "react-dom/server";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { campuses } from "@/data/fixtures/campuses";
import { CodeStep, EmailStep, ProfileStep, SignedInNote, SignInUnavailable } from "./SignInSteps";

// The forms only need something to post to; the actions have their own tests.
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/actions", () => ({
  cancelSignIn: async () => {},
  completeProfile: async () => {},
  resendCode: async () => {},
  signInAsSample: async () => {},
  startSignIn: async () => {},
  verifyCode: async () => {},
}));

const samples = [
  { email: "student@eduvos.com", label: "Student, Midrand" },
  { email: "admin@eduvos.com", label: "Admin" },
];

const hiddenNext = (container: HTMLElement) =>
  [...container.querySelectorAll<HTMLInputElement>('input[type="hidden"][name="next"]')].map((input) => input.value);

describe("step 1, the email (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("asks for an Eduvos email and the privacy policy box, then Send code", () => {
    render(<EmailStep next={null} email="" consented={false} errors={[]} samples={null} />);
    const email = screen.getByLabelText("Your Eduvos email address");
    expect(email).toHaveAttribute("type", "email");
    expect(email).toHaveAttribute("autocomplete", "email");
    expect(email).toHaveAttribute("inputmode", "email");
    expect(email).toHaveAccessibleDescription("We'll send you a 6-digit code to sign in.");
    expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "I agree to the privacy policy" })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "privacy policy" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/privacy");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Send code" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Sign in as a sample account" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows errors in a summary and under their fields, keeping what was typed", () => {
    render(
      <EmailStep
        next={null}
        email="thandi@gmail.com"
        consented
        errors={[{ message: "Use your Eduvos email address.", fieldId: "signin-email" }]}
        samples={null}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const email = screen.getByLabelText("Your Eduvos email address");
    expect(email).toHaveValue("thandi@gmail.com");
    expect(email).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(email).toHaveAccessibleDescription(
      "We'll send you a 6-digit code to sign in. Error: Use your Eduvos email address.",
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "I agree to the privacy policy" })).toBeChecked();
  });

  it("offers the sample accounts in sample mode (spec 2.8), each posting its email", () => {
    render(<EmailStep next={null} email="" consented={false} errors={[]} samples={samples} />);
    const buttons = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Sign in as a sample account" })).getAllByRole("button");
    expect(buttons.map((button) => button.textContent)).toEqual(["Student, Midrand", "Admin"]);
    expect(buttons[0]).toHaveAttribute("name", "email");
    expect(buttons[0]).toHaveAttribute("value", "student@eduvos.com");
  });

  it("carries next through both of its forms, and nothing when there's none", () => {
    const { container, unmount } = render(
      <EmailStep next="/listings/l-001" email="" consented={false} errors={[]} samples={samples} />,
    );
    expect(hiddenNext(container)).toEqual(["/listings/l-001", "/listings/l-001"]);
    unmount();
    const again = render(<EmailStep next={null} email="" consented={false} errors={[]} samples={samples} />);
    expect(hiddenNext(again.container)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("step 2, the code (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  const props = { next: null, maskedEmail: "t•••@eduvos.com", errors: [], resent: false, sampleCode: null };
  const wrong = [{ message: "That code isn't right. 4 tries left.", fieldId: "signin-code" }];

  it("says where the code went, and asks for it", () => {
    render(<CodeStep {...props} />);
    const code = screen.getByLabelText("6-digit code");
    expect(code).toHaveAttribute("inputmode", "numeric");
    expect(code).toHaveAttribute("autocomplete", "one-time-code");
    expect(code).toHaveAccessibleDescription("We sent a code to t•••@eduvos.com. It lasts 10 minutes.");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Send a new code" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Use a different email" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Sample mode/)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("status")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows the code in sample mode (spec 2.3), and says when a new one was sent", () => {
    render(<CodeStep {...props} sampleCode="042137" resent />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Sample mode: your code is/)).toHaveTextContent("Sample mode: your code is 042137");
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("We sent a new code.");
  });

  it("shows a wrong code's error under the field too", () => {
    render(<CodeStep {...props} errors={wrong} />);
    const code = screen.getByLabelText("6-digit code");
    expect(code).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(code).toHaveAccessibleDescription(
      "We sent a code to t•••@eduvos.com. It lasts 10 minutes. Error: That code isn't right. 4 tries left.",
    );
  });

  it("puts you in the code field, unless there's an error to read first (plan decision 11)", () => {
    expect(renderToString(<CodeStep {...props} />)).toMatch(/<input[^>]*id="signin-code"[^>]*autofocus=""/);
    const withError = renderToString(<CodeStep {...props} errors={wrong} />);
    expect(withError).not.toMatch(/<input[^>]*autofocus=""/);
    expect(withError).toMatch(/<div[^>]*role="alert"[^>]*autofocus=""/);
  });

  it("carries next through all three of its forms", () => {
    const { container } = render(<CodeStep {...props} next="/saved" />);
    expect(hiddenNext(container)).toEqual(["/saved", "/saved", "/saved"]);
  });
});

describe("step 3, first sign-in (accounts spec 3.1)", () => {
  it("asks for a first name and a campus, starting on the campus given", () => {
    render(<ProfileStep next={null} firstName="" campus="pretoria" campuses={campuses} errors={[]} />);
    const name = screen.getByLabelText("Your first name");
    expect(name).toHaveAttribute("autocomplete", "given-name");
    expect(name).toHaveAttribute("maxlength", "40");
    expect(name).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Sellers see it when you message them. 2 to 40 characters.");
    expect(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Your campus" })).toHaveValue("pretoria");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
  });

  it("shows its errors and keeps what was typed", () => {
    render(
      <ProfileStep
        next={null}
        firstName="A"
        campus=""
        campuses={campuses}
        errors={[
          { message: "Your first name must be 2 to 40 characters.", fieldId: "signin-first-name" },
          { message: "Choose your campus.", fieldId: "signin-campus" },
        ]}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Your first name")).toHaveValue("A");
    const campus = screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Your campus" });
    expect(campus).toHaveValue("");
    expect(campus).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(campus).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Error: Choose your campus.");
    expect(within(screen.getByRole("alert")).getAllByRole("link")).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});

describe("the other states (accounts spec 2.1 and 3.1)", () => {

    expect(screen.getByText(/You're signed in as/)).toHaveTextContent("You're signed in as student@eduvos.com.");

});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the form parts**

Create `components/forms/ErrorSummary.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";

/**
 * What went wrong on the last post (accounts spec 6), each message linking to its field. On a page load, which is what
 * a post without the app's JavaScript gives, the browser focuses it (autoFocus). With the JavaScript, React only
 * autofocuses form controls, so role="alert" announces it instead (accounts plan decision 11).
 */
export function ErrorSummary({ errors }: { errors: readonly FormError[] }) {
  if (errors.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <div role="alert" tabIndex={-1} autoFocus aria-labelledby="form-summary-title" className="form-summary">
      <h2 id="form-summary-title" className="form-summary-title">
        There&apos;s a problem
      </h2>
      <ul className="form-summary-list">
        {errors.map((error) => (
          <li key={`${error.fieldId}:${error.message}`}>
            {error.fieldId ? <a href={`#${error.fieldId}`}>{error.message}</a> : error.message}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `components/forms/FieldError.tsx`:

```tsx
/** The message under a field (accounts spec 3.1), joined to it by aria-describedby. Nothing when there's no error. */
export function FieldError({ id, message }: { id: string; message: string | undefined }) {
  if (!message) return null;
  return (
    <p id={id} className="form-error">
      <span className="sr-only">Error:</span> {message}
    </p>
  );
}

/** A field's aria-describedby: the ids it's given that aren't empty, or nothing at all. */
export function describedBy(...ids: Array<string | false | null | undefined>): string | undefined {
  const joined = ids.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  return joined || undefined;
}
```

```tsx
import type { Campus } from "@/lib/types";

type CampusSelectProps = {
  id: string;
  /** The campus slug to start on, or "" for "Choose your campus". */
  defaultValue: string;
  campuses: readonly Pick<Campus, "slug" | "name">[];
  invalid: boolean;
  describedBy: string | undefined;
};

/** The 13 campuses as a select (accounts spec 3.1 and 3.2). It posts as `campus`. */
export function CampusSelect({ id, defaultValue, campuses, invalid, describedBy }: CampusSelectProps) {
  return (
    <select
      id={id}
      name="campus"
      defaultValue={defaultValue}
      required
      aria-invalid={invalid ? true : undefined}
      aria-describedby={describedBy}
      className="form-input form-select"
    >
      <option value="">Choose your campus</option>
      {campuses.map((campus) => (
        <option key={campus.slug} value={campus.slug}>
          {campus.name}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the sign-in view**

Create `lib/auth/sign-in-view.ts`:

```ts
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { ALL_CAMPUSES, CAMPUS_COOKIE, parseCampusChoice } from "@/lib/campus";
import { getCampuses } from "@/lib/data";
import { getSampleEmail, getSignInAttempt } from "@/lib/data/sign-in";
import { isSampleMode } from "./config";
import { readCookieValue, SIGN_IN_COOKIE } from "./cookies";
import { maskEmail } from "./email";
import { formErrors, SIGN_IN_FIELDS, type FormError } from "./messages";
import { hashToken } from "./secrets";
import { getViewer } from "./session";

/** What the sign-in page shows this browser (spec 3.1). */
export type SignInView =
  | { step: "unavailable" }
  | { step: "signed-in"; email: string }
  | { step: "email"; email: string; consented: boolean; errors: FormError[] }
  | { step: "code"; maskedEmail: string; errors: FormError[]; resent: boolean; sampleCode: string | null }
  | { step: "profile"; firstName: string; campus: string; errors: FormError[] };

/**
 * Signed in already, or the step this browser's attempt is on, with its errors and what was typed (spec 3.1, plan
 * decision 3). Request-time: call it inside <Suspense>.
 */
export async function getSignInView(): Promise<SignInView> {
  if (!isSampleMode()) return { step: "unavailable" };
  const viewer = await getViewer();
  if (viewer) return { step: "signed-in", email: viewer.email };
  const token = await readCookieValue(SIGN_IN_COOKIE);
  // Whether the attempt has run out is checked against the clock (decision 26).
  await connection();
  const attempt = token ? await getSignInAttempt(hashToken(token), new Date()) : null;
  if (!attempt) return { step: "email", email: "", consented: false, errors: [] };
  const errors = formErrors(attempt.errors, SIGN_IN_FIELDS, attempt.wrongTries);
  if (attempt.step === "code") {
    const sent = await getSampleEmail(attempt.key);
    return {
      step: "code",
      maskedEmail: maskEmail(attempt.email),
      errors,
      resent: attempt.resent,
      sampleCode: sent?.code ?? null,
    };
  }
  if (attempt.step === "profile") {
    return { step: "profile", firstName: attempt.firstName, campus: attempt.campusSlug || (await chosenCampus()), errors };
  }
  return { step: "email", email: attempt.email, consented: attempt.consentedAt !== null, errors };
}

/** The campus chosen on this device, for step 3's select to start on (spec 2.6), or "" for none. */
async function chosenCampus(): Promise<string> {
  const [store, campuses] = await Promise.all([cookies(), getCampuses()]);
  const choice = parseCampusChoice(store.get(CAMPUS_COOKIE)?.value, campuses);
  return choice === ALL_CAMPUSES ? "" : choice;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the steps**

Create `components/auth/SignInSteps.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { CampusSelect } from "@/components/forms/CampusSelect";
import { ErrorSummary } from "@/components/forms/ErrorSummary";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { cancelSignIn, completeProfile, resendCode, signInAsSample, startSignIn, verifyCode } from "@/lib/auth/actions";
import { errorFor, SIGN_IN_FIELDS, type FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import type { Campus } from "@/lib/types";

// The sign-in card's states (accounts spec 3.1). Server Components: every form posts to a Server Action, so each step
// works before the app's JavaScript arrives (plan decision 2).

const FIELDS = SIGN_IN_FIELDS;

export type SampleSignIn = { email: string; label: string };

/** Carries `next` through a step's post, so the last step can follow it. The server checks it again (spec 2.10). */
function NextField({ next }: { next: string | null }) {
  return next ? <input type="hidden" name="next" value={next} /> : null;
}

type EmailStepProps = {
  next: string | null;
  email: string;
  consented: boolean;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  /** The sample accounts, in sample mode only (spec 2.8). */
  samples: readonly SampleSignIn[] | null;
};

/** Step 1: your Eduvos email and the privacy policy box; in sample mode, the sample accounts too. */
export function EmailStep({ next, email, consented, errors, samples }: EmailStepProps) {
  const emailError = errorFor(errors, FIELDS.email);
  const consentError = errorFor(errors, FIELDS.consent);
  return (
    <div className="signin-card">
      <ErrorSummary errors={errors} />
      <form action={startSignIn} noValidate className="form-stack">
        <NextField next={next} />
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor={FIELDS.email} className="form-label">
            Your Eduvos email address
          </label>
          <p id="signin-email-hint" className="form-hint">
            We&apos;ll send you a 6-digit code to sign in.
          </p>
          <FieldError id="signin-email-error" message={emailError} />
          <input
            id={FIELDS.email}
            name="email"
            type="email"
            autoComplete="email"
            inputMode="email"
            spellCheck={false}
            required
            maxLength={254}
            defaultValue={email}
            aria-invalid={emailError ? true : undefined}
            aria-describedby={describedBy("signin-email-hint", emailError && "signin-email-error")}
            className="form-input"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="form-field">
          <FieldError id="signin-consent-error" message={consentError} />
          <div className="form-check">
            <input
              id={FIELDS.consent}
              name="consent"
              type="checkbox"
              value="yes"
              required
              defaultChecked={consented}
              aria-invalid={consentError ? true : undefined}
              aria-describedby={describedBy(consentError && "signin-consent-error")}
              className="form-checkbox"
            />
            <label htmlFor={FIELDS.consent}>
              I agree to the <Link href="/privacy">privacy policy</Link>
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
          Send code
        </button>
      </form>
      {samples && <SampleAccounts next={next} samples={samples} />}
    </div>
  );
}

/** "Sign in as a sample account" (spec 2.8): one form, one button per account, each posting its email. */
function SampleAccounts({ next, samples }: { next: string | null; samples: readonly SampleSignIn[] }) {
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="signin-samples-title" className="signin-samples">
      <p className="signin-or" aria-hidden="true">
        or
      </p>
      <h2 id="signin-samples-title" className="signin-samples-title">
        Sign in as a sample account
      </h2>
      <p className="form-hint">Sample mode only: straight in, no code.</p>
      <form action={signInAsSample} className="signin-sample-list">
        <NextField next={next} />
        {samples.map((sample) => (
          <button
            key={sample.email}
            type="submit"
            name="email"
            value={sample.email}
            className="btn btn-outline text-navy"
          >
            {sample.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}

type CodeStepProps = {
  next: string | null;
  maskedEmail: string;
  errors: readonly FormError[];
  resent: boolean;
  /** The code itself, in sample mode only (spec 2.3). */
  sampleCode: string | null;
};

/** Step 2: the 6-digit code, with Send a new code and Use a different email. */
export function CodeStep({ next, maskedEmail, errors, resent, sampleCode }: CodeStepProps) {
  const codeError = errorFor(errors, FIELDS.code);
  return (
    <div className="signin-card">
      <ErrorSummary errors={errors} />
      {resent && (
        <p role="status" className="form-notice">
          We sent a new code.
        </p>
      )}
      <p id="signin-code-sent" className="signin-lede">
        We sent a code to <strong>{maskedEmail}</strong>. It lasts 10 minutes.
      </p>
      {sampleCode && (
        <p className="signin-sample-code">
          Sample mode: your code is <strong>{sampleCode}</strong>
        </p>
      )}
      <form action={verifyCode} noValidate className="form-stack">
        <NextField next={next} />
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor={FIELDS.code} className="form-label">
            6-digit code
          </label>
          <FieldError id="signin-code-error" message={codeError} />
          {/* Arriving at step 2 puts you in the field, with or without the app's JavaScript. After an error, the
              summary is focused instead (plan decision 11). */}
          <input
            id={FIELDS.code}
            name="code"
            type="text"
            inputMode="numeric"
            autoComplete="one-time-code"
            required
            autoFocus={errors.length === 0}
            aria-invalid={codeError ? true : undefined}
            aria-describedby={describedBy("signin-code-sent", codeError && "signin-code-error")}
            className="form-input form-input-code"
          />
        </div>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
          Sign in
        </button>
      </form>
      <div className="signin-secondary">
        <form action={resendCode}>
          <NextField next={next} />
          <button type="submit" className="link-button">
            Send a new code
          </button>
        </form>
        <form action={cancelSignIn}>
          <NextField next={next} />
          <button type="submit" className="link-button">
            Use a different email
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

type ProfileStepProps = {
  next: string | null;
  firstName: string;
  /** The campus slug to start on, or "". */
  campus: string;
  campuses: readonly Pick<Campus, "slug" | "name">[];
  errors: readonly FormError[];
};

/** Step 3, a first sign-in only (spec 2.6): your first name and your campus. */
export function ProfileStep({ next, firstName, campus, campuses, errors }: ProfileStepProps) {
  const nameError = errorFor(errors, FIELDS.firstName);
  const campusError = errorFor(errors, FIELDS.campus);
  return (
    <div className="signin-card">
      <ErrorSummary errors={errors} />
      <p className="signin-lede">First time on Vossie? Tell us two things and you&apos;re in.</p>
      <form action={completeProfile} noValidate className="form-stack">
        <NextField next={next} />
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor={FIELDS.firstName} className="form-label">
            Your first name
          </label>
          <p id="signin-first-name-hint" className="form-hint">
            Sellers see it when you message them. 2 to 40 characters.
          </p>
          <FieldError id="signin-first-name-error" message={nameError} />
          <input
            id={FIELDS.firstName}
            name="firstName"
            type="text"
            autoComplete="given-name"
            required
            maxLength={40}
            defaultValue={firstName}
            aria-invalid={nameError ? true : undefined}
            aria-describedby={describedBy("signin-first-name-hint", nameError && "signin-first-name-error")}
            className="form-input"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor={FIELDS.campus} className="form-label">
            Your campus
          </label>
          <FieldError id="signin-campus-error" message={campusError} />
          <CampusSelect
            id={FIELDS.campus}
            defaultValue={campus}
            campuses={campuses}
            invalid={Boolean(campusError)}
            describedBy={describedBy(campusError && "signin-campus-error")}
          />
        </div>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
          Continue
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Signed in already (spec 3.1, plan decision 10): who as, and the way to the account. The back link sits below. */
export function SignedInNote({ email }: { email: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="signin-card">
      <p className="signin-lede">
        You&apos;re signed in as <strong>{email}</strong>.
      </p>
      <Link href="/account" prefetch={false} className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
        Your account
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Sample mode is off and there's no backend yet (spec 2.1, plan decision 17). */
export function SignInUnavailable() {
  return (
    <div className="signin-card">
      <p className="signin-lede">
        Sign-in isn&apos;t available yet. You can still browse every campus and save listings on this device.
      </p>
      <Link href="/browse" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
        Browse
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Write the card and its skeleton**

Create `components/auth/SignIn.tsx`:

```tsx
import { isSampleMode } from "@/lib/auth/config";
import { getSignInView } from "@/lib/auth/sign-in-view";
import { getCampuses } from "@/lib/data";
import { getSampleSignIns } from "@/lib/data/accounts";
import { CodeStep, EmailStep, ProfileStep, SignedInNote, SignInUnavailable } from "./SignInSteps";

/** The sign-in card for this browser's state (accounts spec 3.1). Request-time: it sits inside <Suspense> on /login. */
export async function SignIn({ next }: { next: string | null }) {
  const view = await getSignInView();
  switch (view.step) {
    case "unavailable":
      return <SignInUnavailable />;
    case "signed-in":
      return <SignedInNote email={view.email} />;
    case "code":
      return (
        <CodeStep
          next={next}
          maskedEmail={view.maskedEmail}
          errors={view.errors}
          resent={view.resent}
          sampleCode={view.sampleCode}
        />
      );
    case "profile":
      return (
        <ProfileStep
          next={next}
          firstName={view.firstName}
          campus={view.campus}
          campuses={await getCampuses()}
          errors={view.errors}
        />
      );
    case "email":
      return (
        <EmailStep
          next={next}
          email={view.email}
          consented={view.consented}
          errors={view.errors}
          samples={isSampleMode() ? await getSampleSignIns() : null}
        />
      );
  }
}
```

```tsx
/** The card's room while this browser's step streams in, so the back link and the footer don't jump (spec 3.1). */
export function SignInSkeleton() {
  return <div className="signin-card signin-skeleton" aria-hidden="true" />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 12: The page**

Replace `app/login/page.tsx` with:

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { SignIn } from "@/components/auth/SignIn";
import { SignInSkeleton } from "@/components/auth/SignInSkeleton";
import { safeNextPath } from "@/lib/next-path";

/** Signing in belongs to one visitor, so search engines leave it out (accounts spec 3.1). */
export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Sign in", robots: { index: false } };

/**
 * Sign in (accounts spec 3.1). The heading is prerendered; the card for this browser's state and the link back to
 * `next` render per request inside <Suspense>.
 */
export default function LoginPage({ searchParams }: PageProps<"/login">) {
  return (
    <div className="page signin-page">
      <h1 className="signin-title">Sign in</h1>
      <Suspense fallback={<SignInSkeleton />}>
        <SignInForVisitor searchParams={searchParams} />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function SignInForVisitor({ searchParams }: Pick<PageProps<"/login">, "searchParams">) {
  const next = safeNextPath((await searchParams).next);
  return (
    <>
      <SignIn next={next} />
      {/* Back to where the visitor came from, when that's a path on this site (browse and listing spec 5.9). */}
      {next && (
        <p className="signin-back">
          <Link href={next}>{next.startsWith("/listings/") ? "Back to the listing" : "Go back"}</Link>
        </p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Read the docs**

Read Step 2 of `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/authentication-with-cache-components.md` again, especially "Keep the session read out of a layout's top level": the layout passes `<Suspense>`-wrapped slots, and never awaits the session itself. Passing a Server Component as a prop to a Client Component is the "slot" pattern in `05-server-and-client-components.md` under `01-getting-started/`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the account icon's test**

Create `components/header/AccountLink.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { AccountLink, HeaderAccount } from "./AccountLink";

const viewer = vi.hoisted(() => ({ account: null as { id: string } | null, failing: false }));

vi.mock("@/lib/auth/session", () => ({
  getViewer: async () => {
    if (viewer.failing) throw new Error("The session store didn't answer");
    return viewer.account;
  },
}));

afterEach(() => {
  viewer.account = null;
  viewer.failing = false;
});

describe("the header's account icon (accounts spec 3.4)", () => {
  it("says Sign in and goes to sign-in when signed out", () => {
    render(<AccountLink signedIn={false} />);
    const link = screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Sign in" });
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute("href", "/login");
    // Fourth in the header's reveal stagger (spec section 6.2).
    expect(link.style.getPropertyValue("--i")).toBe("3");
  });

  it("says Your account and goes to the account when signed in", () => {
    render(<AccountLink signedIn />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Your account" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/account");
  });

  it("follows the session", async () => {
    viewer.account = { id: "acc-student" };
    render(await HeaderAccount());
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Your account" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("counts a failed session lookup as signed out", async () => {
    viewer.failing = true;
    render(await HeaderAccount());
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Sign in" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the account icon**

Create `components/header/AccountLink.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import type { CSSProperties } from "react";
import { UserIcon } from "@/components/icons";
import { getViewer } from "@/lib/auth/session";

/**
 * The header's account icon (accounts spec 3.4): "Sign in" to /login when signed out, "Your account" to /account when
 * signed in. The layout renders it on the server and hands it to SiteHeader, so the header knows the session without
 * client code or a readable cookie (plan decision 1). No prefetch, like the Saved link: the header is on every page.
 */
export function AccountLink({ signedIn }: { signedIn: boolean }) {
  // --i is the header's reveal stagger: fourth in its group, as before (spec section 6.2).
  return (
    <Link
      href={signedIn ? "/account" : "/login"}
      prefetch={false}
      aria-label={signedIn ? "Your account" : "Sign in"}
      className="hdr-item icon-btn"
      style={{ "--i": 3 } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <UserIcon />
    </Link>
  );
}

/** The icon for this request's session. Request-time, so the layout puts it inside <Suspense>. */
export async function HeaderAccount() {
  // A failed lookup counts as signed out rather than breaking the header.
  const account = await getViewer().catch(() => null);
  return <AccountLink signedIn={account !== null} />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the menu's account rows**

Create `components/menu/MenuAccount.tsx`:

```tsx
import { NavLink } from "@/components/ui/NavLink";
import { signOut } from "@/lib/auth/actions";
import { getViewer } from "@/lib/auth/session";

/**
 * The menu's account rows (accounts spec 3.4): "Sign in" when signed out; "Your account" and Sign out when signed in.
 * Rendered on the server and handed to MenuDrawer, like the header's icon (plan decision 1). Sign out is a plain form
 * posting to a Server Action; the menu closes as it's pressed (plan decision 14).
 */
export function MenuAccountLinks({ signedIn }: { signedIn: boolean }) {
  if (!signedIn) {
    return (
      <li>
        <NavLink href="/login">Sign in</NavLink>
      </li>
    );
  }
  return (
    <>
      <li>
        <NavLink href="/account">Your account</NavLink>
      </li>
      <li>
        <form action={signOut}>
          <button type="submit" className="menu-link-button menu-signout">
            Sign out
          </button>
        </form>
      </li>
    </>
  );
}

/** The rows for this request's session. Request-time, so the layout puts them inside <Suspense>. */
export async function MenuAccount() {
  const account = await getViewer().catch(() => null);
  return <MenuAccountLinks signedIn={account !== null} />;
}
```

The homepage shows "You're signed out" and "Your account has been deleted" (spec 2.13, plan decision 12) through the existing toast region. The one new client component lives on the homepage only.

Create `components/ui/Flash.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { FLASH_COOKIE } from "@/lib/flash";
import { getToast } from "@/lib/toast";
import { FlashContent } from "./Flash";
import { FlashToast } from "./FlashToast";

const flash = vi.hoisted(() => ({ code: undefined as string | undefined }));

vi.mock("next/headers", () => ({
  cookies: async () => ({
    get: (name: string) => (name === "vossie_flash" && flash.code ? { value: flash.code } : undefined),
  }),
}));

afterEach(() => {
  flash.code = undefined;
});

describe("flash toasts (accounts spec 2.13, plan decision 12)", () => {
  it("shows the message as a toast, and deletes the cookie so it shows once", () => {
    document.cookie = `${FLASH_COOKIE}=signed-out; Path=/`;
    render(<FlashToast message="You're signed out" />);
    expect(getToast()?.message).toBe("You're signed out");
    expect(document.cookie).not.toContain(FLASH_COOKIE);
  });

  it("reads the code on the server and shows its words", async () => {
    flash.code = "deleted";
    render((await FlashContent())!);
    expect(getToast()?.message).toBe("Your account has been deleted");
  });

  it("shows nothing for a code it doesn't know, or none", async () => {
    flash.code = "<b>hi</b>";
    expect(await FlashContent()).toBeNull();
    flash.code = undefined;
    expect(await FlashContent()).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the toast and its reader**

Create `components/ui/FlashToast.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { preferenceCookie } from "@/lib/cookies";
import { FLASH_COOKIE } from "@/lib/flash";
import { showToast } from "@/lib/toast";

/**
 * Shows a Server Action's one-off confirmation in the toast region (accounts plan decision 12), then deletes the flash
 * cookie so it shows once. The words come from the server, chosen from a fixed list by the cookie's code.
 */
export function FlashToast({ message }: { message: string }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    showToast(message);
    document.cookie = preferenceCookie(FLASH_COOKIE, "", window.location.protocol === "https:");
  }, [message]);
  return null;
}
```

Create `components/ui/Flash.tsx`:

```tsx
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { FLASH_COOKIE, flashMessage } from "@/lib/flash";
import { FlashToast } from "./FlashToast";

/**
 * The toast a Server Action asked for before redirecting here (accounts plan decision 12). It reads the flash cookie
 * per request, so it sits in its own <Suspense>, and it takes no room on the page.
 */
export function Flash() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <FlashContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

export async function FlashContent() {
  const message = flashMessage((await cookies()).get(FLASH_COOKIE)?.value);
  return message ? <FlashToast message={message} /> : null;
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { campuses } from "@/data/fixtures/campuses";
import type { FormStateRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import type { Account } from "@/lib/types";
import { AccountView, detailsForm } from "./AccountView";

// The forms only need something to post to; the actions have their own tests.
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/actions", () => ({ signOut: async () => {}, updateDetails: async () => {} }));

const account: Account = {
  id: "acc-student",
  email: "student@eduvos.com",
  firstName: "Thandi",
  campusSlug: "midrand",
  roles: [],
  seller: null,
  createdAt: "2026-09-01T07:30:00.000Z",
};

const state = (fields: Partial<FormStateRow>): FormStateRow => ({
  key: "session-key:details",
  sessionKey: "session-key",
  form: "details",
  errors: [],
  values: {},
  notice: null,
  expiresAt: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  ...fields,
});

function renderAccount(who: Account = account, formState: FormStateRow | null = null) {
  return render(<AccountView account={who} campuses={campuses} details={detailsForm(who, formState)} />);
}

describe("Your account (accounts spec 3.2)", () => {
  it("shows the first name, the email, the campus and when they joined", () => {
    renderAccount();
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 2, name: "Thandi" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Email").nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("student@eduvos.com");
    expect(screen.getByText("Campus").nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("Midrand");
    expect(screen.getByText("Member since September 2026")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Hub role")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("starts Edit details on the account's details", () => {
    renderAccount();
    const edit = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Edit details" });
    expect(within(edit).getByLabelText("Your first name")).toHaveValue("Thandi");
    expect(within(edit).getByRole("combobox", { name: "Your campus" })).toHaveValue("midrand");
    expect(within(edit).getByRole("button", { name: "Save changes" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("status")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("says when the details were saved (plan decision 13)", () => {
    renderAccount(account, state({ notice: "saved" }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent("Your details are saved.");
  });

  it("shows what was typed and what's wrong after a failed save", () => {
    renderAccount(account, state({ errors: ["first-name-length", "campus-missing"], values: { firstName: "T", campus: "" } }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const name = screen.getByLabelText("Your first name");
    expect(name).toHaveValue("T");
    expect(name).toHaveAccessibleDescription(
      "Sellers see it when you message them. 2 to 40 characters. Error: Your first name must be 2 to 40 characters.",
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Your campus" })).toHaveValue("");
  });

  it("links to Saved, selling and privacy settings, and offers Sign out", () => {
    renderAccount();
    const pages = screen.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Your account's pages" });
    expect(within(pages).getAllByRole("link").map((link) => [link.textContent, link.getAttribute("href")])).toEqual([
      ["Saved", "/saved"],
      ["Start selling", "/sell"],
      ["Privacy settings", "/account/privacy"],
    ]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign out" })).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
  });

  it("points a seller at their shop, and says when it's waiting or paused (plan decision 16)", () => {
    const approved = renderAccount({ ...account, seller: { status: "approved", sellerSlug: "tech-corner" } });
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Your shop" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sellers/tech-corner");
    approved.unmount();
    const pending = renderAccount({ ...account, seller: { status: "pending", sellerSlug: null } });
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: /^Your shop\s*,\s*Waiting for the Hub's approval$/ })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/sell",
    );
    pending.unmount();
    renderAccount({ ...account, seller: { status: "paused", sellerSlug: "tech-corner" } });
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: /^Your shop\s*,\s*Paused$/ })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/sellers/tech-corner");
  });

  it("names a mentor's or an admin's Hub role (plan decision 16)", () => {
    renderAccount({ ...account, roles: ["admin"] });
    expect(screen.getByText("Hub role").nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent("Admin");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the page's parts**

Create `components/account/AccountView.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { CampusSelect } from "@/components/forms/CampusSelect";
import { ErrorSummary } from "@/components/forms/ErrorSummary";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { ChevronRightIcon } from "@/components/icons";
import { signOut, updateDetails } from "@/lib/auth/actions";
import { DETAILS_FIELDS } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { errorFor, formErrors, type FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import type { FormStateRow } from "@/lib/data/rows";
import { sastMonthYear } from "@/lib/time";
import type { Account, Campus, StaffRole } from "@/lib/types";

export type DetailsForm = { firstName: string; campus: string; errors: FormError[]; saved: boolean };

/** Edit details' state: after a failed save, what was typed and what's wrong; otherwise the account's own details. */
export function detailsForm(account: Account, state: FormStateRow | null): DetailsForm {
  if (state && state.errors.length > 0) {
    return {
      firstName: state.values.firstName ?? account.firstName,
      campus: state.values.campus ?? account.campusSlug,
      errors: formErrors(state.errors, DETAILS_FIELDS),
      saved: false,
    };
  }
  return { firstName: account.firstName, campus: account.campusSlug, errors: [], saved: state?.notice === "saved" };
}

const ROLE_NAMES: Record<StaffRole, string> = { mentor: "Mentor", admin: "Admin" };

type AccountViewProps = {
  account: Account;
  campuses: readonly Pick<Campus, "slug" | "name">[];
  details: DetailsForm;
};

/** Your account (accounts spec 3.2): who you are, Edit details, the account's pages, and Sign out. */
export function AccountView({ account, campuses, details }: AccountViewProps) {
  const campusName = campuses.find((campus) => campus.slug === account.campusSlug)?.name ?? "None chosen";
  const nameError = errorFor(details.errors, DETAILS_FIELDS.firstName);
  const campusError = errorFor(details.errors, DETAILS_FIELDS.campus);
  return (
    <div className="account-body">
      <section aria-labelledby="account-you-title" className="account-card">
        <h2 id="account-you-title" className="account-name">
          {account.firstName}
        </h2>
        <dl className="account-facts">
          <div>
            <dt>Email</dt>
            <dd>{account.email}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt>Campus</dt>
            <dd>{campusName}</dd>
          </div>
          {account.roles.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <dt>Hub role</dt>
              <dd>{account.roles.map((role) => ROLE_NAMES[role]).join(" and ")}</dd>
            </div>
          )}
        </dl>
        <p className="account-since">Member since {sastMonthYear(account.createdAt)}</p>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="account-edit-title" className="account-section">
        <h2 id="account-edit-title" className="account-section-title">
          Edit details
        </h2>
        {details.saved && (
          <p role="status" className="form-notice">
            Your details are saved.
          </p>
        )}
        <ErrorSummary errors={details.errors} />
        <form action={updateDetails} noValidate className="form-stack">
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor={DETAILS_FIELDS.firstName} className="form-label">
              Your first name
            </label>
            <p id="details-first-name-hint" className="form-hint">
              Sellers see it when you message them. 2 to 40 characters.
            </p>
            <FieldError id="details-first-name-error" message={nameError} />
            <input
              id={DETAILS_FIELDS.firstName}
              name="firstName"
              type="text"
              autoComplete="given-name"
              required
              maxLength={40}
              defaultValue={details.firstName}
              aria-invalid={nameError ? true : undefined}
              aria-describedby={describedBy("details-first-name-hint", nameError && "details-first-name-error")}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor={DETAILS_FIELDS.campus} className="form-label">
              Your campus
            </label>
            <FieldError id="details-campus-error" message={campusError} />
            <CampusSelect
              id={DETAILS_FIELDS.campus}
              defaultValue={details.campus}
              campuses={campuses}
              invalid={Boolean(campusError)}
              describedBy={describedBy(campusError && "details-campus-error")}
            />
          </div>
          <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary form-submit">
            Save changes
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>

      <nav aria-label="Your account's pages">
        <ul className="account-rows">
          <li>
            {/* No prefetch, as in the header: Saved's card chunk would load on every visit here. */}
            <Link href="/saved" prefetch={false} className="account-row">
              Saved
              <ChevronRightIcon />
            </Link>
          </li>
          <li>
            <SellingRow seller={account.seller} />
          </li>
          <li>
            <Link href="/account/privacy" className="account-row">
              Privacy settings
              <ChevronRightIcon />
            </Link>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </nav>

      <form action={signOut}>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-outline text-navy">
          Sign out
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

/** "Start selling" or "Your shop" (spec 3.2, plan decision 16). Stage F points these at its own pages. */
function SellingRow({ seller }: { seller: Account["seller"] }) {
  if (!seller) {
    return (
      <Link href="/sell" className="account-row">
        Start selling
        <ChevronRightIcon />
      </Link>
    );
  }
  const waiting = seller.status === "pending" || !seller.sellerSlug;
  let detail: string | null = null;
  if (waiting) detail = "Waiting for the Hub's approval";
  else if (seller.status === "paused") detail = "Paused";
  return (
    // No prefetch on the storefront: its chunk carries Share and the hearts (decision 53).
    <Link
      href={waiting ? "/sell" : `/sellers/${seller.sellerSlug}`}
      prefetch={waiting ? undefined : false}
      className="account-row"
    >
      <span>
        Your shop
        {detail && (
          <>
            <span className="sr-only">,</span> <span className="account-row-detail">{detail}</span>
          </>
        )}
      </span>
      <ChevronRightIcon />
    </Link>
  );
}
```

```tsx
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The page**

Replace `app/account/page.tsx` with:

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { AccountSkeleton } from "@/components/account/AccountSkeleton";
import { AccountView, detailsForm } from "@/components/account/AccountView";
import { DETAILS_FORM, readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { requireAccount } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { getCampuses } from "@/lib/data";

/** An account page belongs to one person, so search engines leave it out (accounts spec 3.3). */
export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Your account", robots: { index: false } };

/**
 * Your account (accounts spec 3.2). The heading is prerendered; everything else needs the session, so it renders per
 * request inside <Suspense>. A signed-out visitor is sent to sign in, and comes back here afterwards (spec 2.10).
 */
export default function AccountPage() {
  return (
    <div className="page account-page">
      <h1 className="account-title">Your account</h1>
      <Suspense fallback={<AccountSkeleton />}>
        <AccountContent />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function AccountContent() {
  const account = await requireAccount("/account");
  const [campuses, state] = await Promise.all([getCampuses(), readFormState(DETAILS_FORM)]);
  return <AccountView account={account} campuses={campuses} details={detailsForm(account, state)} />;
}
```

```css
/* --- Accounts: your account (accounts plan, Task 10, spec 3.2) --- */
@layer components {
  /* .page and .account-page sit on one element, so this padding is the whole content edge: 16px, then 40px. */
  .account-page {
    padding: 24px 16px 64px;
  }

  .account-title {
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-family: var(--vos-font-display);
    font-size: var(--text-section);
    line-height: 1;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }

  /* One readable column (spec 3.2). */
  .account-body {
    display: grid;
    gap: 36px;
    max-width: 40rem;
    margin-top: 24px;
  }

  .account-card {
    padding: 20px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-panel);
    background: var(--color-panel);
  }

  .account-name {
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-family: var(--vos-font-display);
    font-size: 30px;
    line-height: 1;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  }

  .account-facts {
    display: grid;
    gap: 8px;
    margin-top: 14px;
  }

  .account-facts div {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 6.5rem minmax(0, 1fr);
    gap: 12px;
  }

  /* Muted on the grey panel is 5.4:1. */
  .account-facts dt,
  .account-since {
    color: var(--color-muted);
    font-size: 14px;
  }

  .account-facts dd {
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  }

  .account-since {
    margin-top: 14px;
  }

  .account-section {
    display: grid;
    gap: 16px;
  }

  .account-section-title {
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-family: var(--vos-font-display);
    font-size: 26px;
    line-height: 1;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }

  .account-rows {
    border-top: 1.5px solid var(--color-line);
  }

  .account-rows li {
    border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--color-line);
  }

  .account-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 12px;
    min-height: 56px;
    padding-block: 10px;
    color: var(--color-navy);
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  .account-row svg {
    flex: none;
  }

  .account-row-detail {
    display: block;
    color: var(--color-muted);
    font-size: 13px;
    font-weight: 400;
  }

  @media (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine) {
    .account-row:hover {
      text-decoration: underline;
      text-underline-offset: 3px;
    }
  }

  .account-skeleton-card {
    height: 10.5rem;
    border-radius: var(--radius-panel);
  }

  .account-skeleton-form {
    height: 22rem;
  }

  @media (min-width: 48rem) and (min-height: 30rem) {
    .account-page {
      padding-inline: 40px;
    }

    .account-card {
      padding: 28px;
    }
  }
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { PrivacySettingsView } from "./PrivacySettingsView";

// The form only needs something to post to; the action has its own test.
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/actions", () => ({ deleteAccount: async () => {} }));

const consent = { version: "2026-10-draft", agreedAt: "2026-10-14T08:42:00.000Z" };

describe("Privacy settings (accounts spec 3.3)", () => {
  it("says when the privacy policy was agreed to, in South African time", () => {
    render(<PrivacySettingsView consent={consent} deleteErrors={[]} />);
    const section = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Your consent" });
    expect(within(section).getByText(/You agreed to the/)).toHaveTextContent(
      "You agreed to the privacy policy (draft of October 2026) on 14 October 2026 at 10:42.",
    );
    expect(within(section).getByRole("link", { name: "privacy policy" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/privacy");
  });

  it("says so when there's no record of consent", () => {
    render(<PrivacySettingsView consent={null} deleteErrors={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/no record of you agreeing/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("links to the download (spec 3.3)", () => {
    render(<PrivacySettingsView consent={consent} deleteErrors={[]} />);
    const link = screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Download your data" });
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute("href", "/account/privacy/export");
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute("download", "vossie-data.json");
  });

  it("says what deleting does, and asks for the box before the red button (spec 2.13)", () => {
    render(<PrivacySettingsView consent={consent} deleteErrors={[]} />);
    const section = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Delete your account" });
    expect(within(section).getAllByRole("listitem")).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(within(section).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "I understand this can't be undone" })).not.toBeChecked();
    const button = within(section).getByRole("button", { name: "Delete my account" });
    expect(button).toHaveAttribute("type", "submit");
    expect(button).toHaveClass("btn-danger");
  });

  it("shows the box's error in the summary and beside the box", () => {
    render(
      <PrivacySettingsView
        consent={consent}
        deleteErrors={[{ message: "Tick the box to confirm you understand.", fieldId: "delete-confirm" }]}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("Tick the box to confirm you understand.");
    const box = screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "I understand this can't be undone" });
    expect(box).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(box).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Error: Tick the box to confirm you understand.");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the export's test**

Create `app/account/privacy/export/route.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/lib/auth/cookies";
import { hashToken } from "@/lib/auth/secrets";
import { createSession } from "@/lib/data/sessions";
import { resetSampleStore } from "@/lib/data/store";
import { redirectOf, resetRequest, type FakeRequest } from "@/tests/helpers/next-request";
import { GET } from "./route";

const request = vi.hoisted((): FakeRequest => ({ cookies: new Map(), sent: new Map(), proto: "http" }));

vi.mock("next/headers", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextHeaders(request));
vi.mock("next/navigation", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextNavigation());
vi.mock("next/server", async () => (await import("@/tests/helpers/next-request")).nextServer());

const NOW = new Date("2026-10-14T08:42:00.000Z");

beforeEach(() => {
  resetSampleStore();
  resetRequest(request);
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
});

describe("the data export (accounts spec 3.3, plan decision 20)", () => {
  it("downloads the signed-in account's data as vossie-data.json, never cached", async () => {
    await createSession({
      key: hashToken("token-1"),
      accountId: "acc-student",
      createdAt: NOW,
      expiresAt: new Date("2026-11-13T08:42:00.000Z"),
    });
    request.cookies.set(SESSION_COOKIE, "token-1");
    const response = await GET();
    expect(response.headers.get("content-type")).toBe("application/json; charset=utf-8");
    expect(response.headers.get("content-disposition")).toBe('attachment; filename="vossie-data.json"');
    expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    const data = await response.json();
    expect(data).toMatchObject({
      exportedAt: NOW.toISOString(),
      account: { email: "student@eduvos.com", firstName: "Thandi", campus: "midrand" },
      consents: [{ policy: "2026-10-draft" }],
      sessions: [{ signedInAt: NOW.toISOString() }],
    });
    const text = JSON.stringify(data);
    expect(text).not.toContain("token-1");
    expect(text).not.toContain(hashToken("token-1"));
  });

  it("sends a signed-out visitor to sign in first", async () => {
    expect(await redirectOf(() => GET())).toBe("/login?next=%2Faccount%2Fprivacy");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the page's parts**

Create `components/account/PrivacySettingsView.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { ErrorSummary } from "@/components/forms/ErrorSummary";
import { describedBy, FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import { deleteAccount } from "@/lib/auth/actions";
import { policyLabel } from "@/lib/auth/config";
import { DELETE_FIELDS } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { errorFor, type FormError } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import { longSastDate, sastClock } from "@/lib/time";
import type { Consent } from "@/lib/types";

type PrivacySettingsViewProps = {
  consent: Consent | null;
  deleteErrors: readonly FormError[];
};

/** Privacy settings (accounts spec 3.3): the consent record, the download, and deleting the account (spec 2.13). */
export function PrivacySettingsView({ consent, deleteErrors }: PrivacySettingsViewProps) {
  const confirmError = errorFor(deleteErrors, DELETE_FIELDS.confirm);
  let consentLine: ReactNode;
  if (consent) {
    consentLine = (
      <p>
        You agreed to the <Link href="/privacy">privacy policy</Link> ({policyLabel(consent.version)}) on{" "}
        {longSastDate(consent.agreedAt)} at {sastClock(consent.agreedAt)}.
      </p>
    );
  } else {
    consentLine = (
      <p>There&apos;s no record of you agreeing to the privacy policy. You&apos;ll be asked when you next sign in.</p>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="account-body">
      <section aria-labelledby="account-privacy-consent-title" className="account-section">
        <h2 id="account-privacy-consent-title" className="account-section-title">
          Your consent
        </h2>
        {consentLine}
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="account-privacy-download-title" className="account-section">
        <h2 id="account-privacy-download-title" className="account-section-title">
          Download your data
        </h2>
        <p>
          A file of what Vossie holds about you: your account, your consent and when you signed in. Once messaging and
          selling open, it will hold your messages, reports and listings too.
        </p>
        {/* A file from a route handler, not a page, so a plain link. */}
        <p>
          <a href="/account/privacy/export" download="vossie-data.json" className="btn btn-outline text-navy">
            Download your data
          </a>
        </p>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="account-privacy-delete-title" className="account-section account-delete">
        <h2 id="account-privacy-delete-title" className="account-section-title">
          Delete your account
        </h2>
        <p>It happens straight away, and it can&apos;t be undone:</p>
        <ul>
          <li>your account goes: your email address, first name and campus;</li>
          <li>you&apos;re signed out on every device;</li>
          <li>your record of consent goes;</li>
          <li>once messaging and selling open, your messages, shop and listings go too.</li>
        </ul>
        <p>Saved listings stay on this device until you clear them.</p>
        <ErrorSummary errors={deleteErrors} />
        <form action={deleteAccount} noValidate className="form-stack">
          <div className="form-field">
            <FieldError id="delete-confirm-error" message={confirmError} />
            <div className="form-check">
              <input
                id={DELETE_FIELDS.confirm}
                name="confirm"
                type="checkbox"
                value="yes"
                required
                aria-invalid={confirmError ? true : undefined}
                aria-describedby={describedBy(confirmError && "delete-confirm-error")}
                className="form-checkbox"
              />
              <label htmlFor={DELETE_FIELDS.confirm}>I understand this can&apos;t be undone</label>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" className="btn btn-danger form-submit">
            Delete my account
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The page and the export**

Create `app/account/privacy/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { AccountSkeleton } from "@/components/account/AccountSkeleton";
import { PrivacySettingsView } from "@/components/account/PrivacySettingsView";
import { ArrowLeftIcon } from "@/components/icons";
import { DELETE_FIELDS, DELETE_FORM, readFormState } from "@/lib/auth/form-state";
import { formErrors } from "@/lib/auth/messages";
import { requireAccount } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { getLatestConsent } from "@/lib/data/accounts";

/** Like every account page, kept out of search engines (accounts spec 3.3). */
export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Privacy settings", robots: { index: false } };

/**
 * Privacy settings (accounts spec 3.3). The heading and the way back are prerendered; the rest needs the session, so it
 * renders per request inside <Suspense>, and a signed-out visitor is sent to sign in first (spec 2.10).
 */
export default function PrivacySettingsPage() {
  return (
    <div className="page account-page">
      <Link href="/account" className="account-crumb">
        <ArrowLeftIcon />
        Back to your account
      </Link>
      <h1 className="account-title">Privacy settings</h1>
      <Suspense fallback={<AccountSkeleton />}>
        <PrivacySettings />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function PrivacySettings() {
  const account = await requireAccount("/account/privacy");
  const [consent, state] = await Promise.all([getLatestConsent(account.id), readFormState(DELETE_FORM)]);
  return <PrivacySettingsView consent={consent} deleteErrors={formErrors(state?.errors ?? [], DELETE_FIELDS)} />;
}
```

Create `app/account/privacy/export/route.ts`:

```ts
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getViewer } from "@/lib/auth/session";
import { exportAccountData } from "@/lib/data/accounts";
import { signInHref } from "@/lib/next-path";

/**
 * "Download your data" (accounts spec 3.3, plan decision 20): the signed-in account's data as vossie-data.json. It
 * reads the session, so it runs per request, and it tells browsers and hosts not to keep a copy. A signed-out visitor
 * is sent to sign in first.
 */
export async function GET(): Promise<Response> {
  const account = await getViewer();
  if (!account) redirect(signInHref("/account/privacy"));
  const data = await exportAccountData(account.id, new Date());
  if (!data) redirect(signInHref("/account/privacy"));
  return new Response(`${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}\n`, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
      "Content-Disposition": 'attachment; filename="vossie-data.json"',
      "Cache-Control": "no-store",
    },
  });
}
```

### Task 13: End-to-end tests for sign-in and accounts

Every flow in spec 8's list, at 390 and 1440 (the header and menu at every size), including the forms with the app's bundle blocked and the checks that no stale page comes back after signing in or out (plan decision 2). Each test that signs up uses its own address; sample accounts are only signed in to (plan decision 22).

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/e2e/accounts.spec.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**

Create `tests/e2e/accounts.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

const project = () => test.info().project.name;

function onlyAt(...projects: string[]) {
  test.skip(!projects.includes(project()), `Checked at ${projects.join(" and ")}`);
}

/**
 * A new address for each test. Every test shares one server and one sample store and runs alongside the others, so a
 * test that signs up never reuses an address (plan decision 22). Sample accounts are only ever signed in to.
 */
function newEmail(): string {
  return `e2e-${project()}-${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}@eduvos.com`;
}

/** The page that's showing: Next keeps earlier pages hidden in the DOM, so look only at what's visible. */
const main = (page: Page) => page.locator("main");
const field = (page: Page, label: string) => page.getByLabel(label, { exact: true }).filter({ visible: true });
const button = (page: Page, name: string) => main(page).getByRole("button", { name, exact: true });

/** The header's account icon (accounts spec 3.4). */
const accountIcon = (page: Page) =>
  page.locator(".site-header").getByRole("link", { name: /^(Sign in|Your account)$/ });

/** The code sample mode shows on step 2 (spec 2.3). */
async function shownCode(page: Page): Promise<string> {
  const note = main(page).getByText(/Sample mode: your code is/).filter({ visible: true });
  await expect(note).toBeVisible();
  return /(\d{6})/.exec((await note.textContent()) ?? "")![1];
}

/** A code that isn't the one shown. */
const wrongCodeFor = (code: string) => (code === "111111" ? "222222" : "111111");

/** Step 1: an email and the privacy box, then Send code. Ends on step 2. */
async function sendCode(page: Page, email: string) {
  await field(page, "Your Eduvos email address").fill(email);
  await field(page, "I agree to the privacy policy").check();
  await button(page, "Send code").click();
  await expect(field(page, "6-digit code")).toBeVisible();
}

/** Step 2, with the code sample mode shows. */
async function enterShownCode(page: Page) {
  await field(page, "6-digit code").fill(await shownCode(page));
  await button(page, "Sign in").click();
}

/** Step 3, a first sign-in only. */
async function finishFirstSignIn(page: Page, firstName = "Ayanda", campus = "Pretoria") {
  await field(page, "Your first name").fill(firstName);
  await field(page, "Your campus").selectOption({ label: campus });
  await button(page, "Continue").click();
}

/** A whole first sign-in with a new email, starting from `path`. */
async function signUp(page: Page, email: string, path = "/login") {
  await page.goto(path);
  await sendCode(page, email);
  await enterShownCode(page);
  await finishFirstSignIn(page);
}

/** A sample account's button (spec 2.8). It lands on /account, or on `next` when the path has one. */
async function signInAsSample(page: Page, label: string, path = "/login") {
  await page.goto(path);
  await button(page, label).click();
}

test.describe("signing in with an email code (accounts spec 2.2 and 3.1)", () => {
  test("a first sign-in from a listing asks for a name and campus, then comes back to the listing", async ({
    page,
    context,
    baseURL,
  }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await context.addCookies([{ name: "vossie_campus", value: "pretoria", url: baseURL! }]);
    await page.goto("/listings/l-001");
    await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Message seller" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001$/);
    const email = newEmail();
    await sendCode(page, email);
    await expect(main(page).getByText(/We sent a code to/).filter({ visible: true })).toContainText(
      `${email[0]}•••@eduvos.com`,
    );
    // Nothing personal goes in the address (spec 2.2).
    expect(page.url()).not.toContain(email.split("@")[0]);
    await enterShownCode(page);
    // Step 3 starts on the campus chosen on this device (spec 2.6).
    await expect(field(page, "Your campus")).toHaveValue("pretoria");
    await field(page, "Your first name").fill("Ayanda");
    await button(page, "Continue").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/listings\/l-001$/);
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Your account");
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAttribute("href", "/account");
  });

  test("an existing account signs straight in, without step 3", async ({ page, baseURL }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    const email = newEmail();
    await signUp(page, email);
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account$/);
    await button(page, "Sign out").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${baseURL}/`);
    await page.goto("/login?next=%2Fsaved");
    await sendCode(page, email);
    await enterShownCode(page);
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/saved$/);
  });

  test("a wrong code says how many tries are left, and the fifth starts again", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    const email = newEmail();
    await page.goto("/login");
    await sendCode(page, email);
    const wrong = wrongCodeFor(await shownCode(page));
    const summary = main(page).getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" });
    for (const left of [4, 3, 2, 1]) {
      await field(page, "6-digit code").fill(wrong);
      await button(page, "Sign in").click();
      await expect(summary).toContainText(`That code isn't right. ${left} ${left === 1 ? "try" : "tries"} left.`);
    }
    await field(page, "6-digit code").fill(wrong);
    await button(page, "Sign in").click();
    await expect(summary).toContainText("Too many tries. Start again.");
    await expect(field(page, "Your Eduvos email address")).toHaveValue(email);
  });

  test("step 1's errors are listed and linked to their fields, and keep what was typed", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await page.goto("/login");
    await button(page, "Send code").click();
    const summary = main(page).getByRole("alert", { name: "There's a problem" });
    await expect(summary.getByRole("link")).toHaveText([
      "Enter your email address.",
      "Tick the box to agree to the privacy policy.",
    ]);
    await field(page, "Your Eduvos email address").fill("someone@gmail.com");
    await field(page, "I agree to the privacy policy").check();
    await button(page, "Send code").click();
    await expect(summary.getByRole("link")).toHaveText(["Use your Eduvos email address."]);
    await expect(field(page, "Your Eduvos email address")).toHaveValue("someone@gmail.com");
    await expect(field(page, "I agree to the privacy policy")).toBeChecked();
  });

  test("Send a new code says so, and Use a different email starts again", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await page.goto("/login");
    await sendCode(page, newEmail());
    await button(page, "Send a new code").click();
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toHaveText("We sent a new code.");
    await button(page, "Use a different email").click();
    await expect(field(page, "Your Eduvos email address")).toHaveValue("");
  });
});

test.describe("the header and the menu (accounts spec 3.4)", () => {
  test("say Sign in when signed out, and Your account and Sign out when signed in", async ({ page, baseURL }) => {
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Sign in");
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAttribute("href", "/login");
    const menuButton = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Menu", exact: true });
    const menu = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Menu" });
    await menuButton.click();
    await expect(menu.getByRole("link", { name: "Sign in" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/login");
    await expect(menu.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign out" })).toHaveCount(0);
    await page.keyboard.press("Escape");

    await signInAsSample(page, "Student, Midrand");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account$/);
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Your account");
    await menuButton.click();
    await expect(menu.getByRole("link", { name: "Your account" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/account");

    // Sign out from the menu: it closes, the homepage says so, and the header follows (plan decisions 12 and 14).
    await menu.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign out" }).click();
    await expect(menu).toBeHidden();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${baseURL}/`);
    await expect(page.locator(".toast")).toHaveText("You're signed out");
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Sign in");
  });
});

test.describe("no stale pages after signing in or out (plan decision 2)", () => {
  test("Back to the sign-in page after signing in shows you're signed in", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await page.goto("/listings/l-001");
    await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Message seller" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001$/);
    await button(page, "Student, Midrand").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/listings\/l-001$/);
    await page.goBack();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001$/);
    await expect(main(page).getByText(/You're signed in as/).filter({ visible: true })).toContainText(
      "student@eduvos.com",
    );
    await expect(field(page, "Your Eduvos email address")).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test("Back to the account page after signing out asks you to sign in", async ({ page, baseURL }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await signInAsSample(page, "Student, Midrand");
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account$/);
    await main(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Saved" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/saved$/);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Menu", exact: true }).click();
    await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Menu" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Sign out" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${baseURL}/`);
    await page.goBack();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/saved$/);
    await page.goBack();
    // The kept copy of /account must not come back: the server sends a signed-out visitor to sign in.
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\?next=%2Faccount$/);
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("heading", { level: 2, name: "Thandi" })).toHaveCount(0);
  });
});

test.describe("your account (accounts spec 3.2 and 3.3)", () => {
  test("shows your details, and Edit details saves them or says what's wrong", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await signUp(page, newEmail());
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account$/);
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("heading", { level: 2, name: "Ayanda" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(main(page).getByText(/^Member since /)).toBeVisible();
    await field(page, "Your first name").fill("Lindiwe");
    await field(page, "Your campus").selectOption({ label: "Durban Umhlanga" });
    await button(page, "Save changes").click();
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toHaveText("Your details are saved.");
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("heading", { level: 2, name: "Lindiwe" })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(main(page).getByText("Campus", { exact: true }).locator("xpath=following-sibling::dd")).toHaveText(
      "Durban Umhlanga",
    );
    await field(page, "Your first name").fill("L");
    await button(page, "Save changes").click();
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("alert")).toContainText("Your first name must be 2 to 40 characters.");
    await expect(field(page, "Your first name")).toHaveValue("L");
  });

  test("Download your data saves vossie-data.json with your account in it and no ids", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await signInAsSample(page, "Mentor");
    await main(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Privacy settings" }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account\/privacy$/);
    await expect(main(page).getByText(/You agreed to the privacy policy/)).toContainText("(draft of October 2026)");
    const [download] = await Promise.all([
      page.waitForEvent("download"),
      main(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Download your data" }).click(),
    ]);
    expect(download.suggestedFilename()).toBe("vossie-data.json");
    const data = JSON.parse(await readFile((await download.path())!, "utf8"));
    expect(data.account).toMatchObject({ email: "mentor@eduvos.com", roles: ["mentor"] });
    expect(data.sessions.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    const session = (await page.context().cookies()).find((cookie) => cookie.name === "vossie_session")!;
    expect(JSON.stringify(data)).not.toContain(session.value);
  });

  test("Delete my account asks for the box, then deletes it and signs you out with a toast (spec 2.13)", async ({
    page,
    baseURL,
  }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    const email = newEmail();
    await signUp(page, email);
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account$/);
    await page.goto("/account/privacy");
    await button(page, "Delete my account").click();
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("alert")).toContainText("Tick the box to confirm you understand.");
    await field(page, "I understand this can't be undone").check();
    await button(page, "Delete my account").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${baseURL}/`);
    await expect(page.locator(".toast")).toHaveText("Your account has been deleted");
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Sign in");
    // The account has gone: the same email starts a first sign-in again.
    await page.goto("/login");
    await sendCode(page, email);
    await enterShownCode(page);
    await expect(field(page, "Your first name")).toBeVisible();
  });
});

});

test.describe("without the app's JavaScript (spec 2.2, plan decisions 2 and 11)", () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    // The app's bundle never arrives. React's small inline script still puts streamed content in place.
    await page.route("**/_next/static/**/*.js", (route) => route.abort());
  });

  test("a whole first sign-in works, the error summary takes focus, and Sign out works", async ({ page, baseURL }) => {

    await page.goto("/login?next=%2Flistings%2Fl-001");
    await sendCode(page, newEmail());
    const code = await shownCode(page);
    await field(page, "6-digit code").fill(wrongCodeFor(code));
    await button(page, "Sign in").click();
    // Without the app's JavaScript a post is a full page load, so the browser focuses the summary (autoFocus).
    const summary = main(page).locator(".form-summary");
    await expect(summary).toContainText("That code isn't right. 4 tries left.");
    await expect(summary).toBeFocused();
    await field(page, "6-digit code").fill(code);
    await button(page, "Sign in").click();
    await finishFirstSignIn(page);
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/listings\/l-001$/);
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Your account");
    await page.goto("/account");
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("heading", { level: 2, name: "Ayanda" })).toBeVisible();
    await button(page, "Sign out").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${baseURL}/`);
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Sign in");
  });

  test("Edit details and Delete my account work", async ({ page, baseURL }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await signUp(page, newEmail());
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account$/);
    await field(page, "Your first name").fill("Zola");
    await button(page, "Save changes").click();
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("status")).toHaveText("Your details are saved.");
    await page.goto("/account/privacy");
    await field(page, "I understand this can't be undone").check();
    await button(page, "Delete my account").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(`${baseURL}/`);
    await expect(accountIcon(page)).toHaveAccessibleName("Sign in");
  });

  test("a sample account's button and the redirect to sign in work", async ({ page }) => {
    onlyAt("phone-390", "desktop-1440");
    await page.goto("/account");
    // Next's streamed redirect is a meta refresh after a second when the app's JavaScript is missing.
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\?next=%2Faccount$/);
    await button(page, "Student, Midrand").click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/account$/);
    await expect(main(page).getByRole("heading", { level: 2, name: "Thandi" })).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```
