If a test fails, look at the trace before changing anything:
- **A stale page** (Back shows step 1 after signing in, or the account page after signing out) is a real bug, not a test problem: Next didn't drop its kept copy. Stop and report it with the trace. Don't change the test to a full page load. The likely fix is a Server Action that changes no cookie (check that sign-in and sign-out set or delete one).
- **The summary isn't focused** with the bundle blocked: report it with the trace. Plan decision 11 depends on the browser honouring `autofocus` on streamed content; the fallback is Lama's call, not a new client component.
- **A strict-mode error** naming two elements: make the locator exact (scope it to `main`, add `exact: true` or `.filter({ visible: true })`), and note it for the plan's "Changes made during the build".

- [ ] **Step 3: Measure the account page's JS, signed in**

In `tests/e2e/performance.spec.ts`, add at the end of the file:

```ts

test("a first visit to the account page, signed in, stays inside the JavaScript budget", async ({
  page,
  context,
  browser,
  baseURL,
}) => {
  test.skip(test.info().project.name !== "phone-390", "Measured once, at phone size");
  // Sign in in a separate browser context, and copy only the session cookie across, so nothing here is cached yet.
  const other = await browser.newContext();
  const signingIn = await other.newPage();
  await signingIn.goto(`${baseURL}/login`);
  await signingIn.locator("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Student, Midrand", exact: true }).click();
  await signingIn.waitForURL(/\/account$/);
  await context.addCookies((await other.cookies()).filter((cookie) => cookie.name === "vossie_session"));
  await other.close();

  let scriptBrotli = 0;
  const pending: Array<Promise<void>> = [];
  page.on("response", (response) => {
    if (response.request().resourceType() !== "script") return;
    pending.push(
      response.body().then((body) => {
        scriptBrotli += brotliCompressSync(body, { params: { [constants.BROTLI_PARAM_QUALITY]: 5 } }).length;
      }),
    );
  });
  await page.goto("/account", { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 2, name: "Thandi" })).toBeVisible();
  await Promise.all(pending);
  console.log(`account page (signed in): ${JSON.stringify({ scriptBrotli })}`);
  expect(scriptBrotli, "JavaScript, brotli as a host serves it").toBeLessThanOrEqual(150 * 1024);
});
```

In `docs/PROGRESS.md`, add a section before "Next", in the style of the earlier ones:

```markdown
## 2026-10-<day>: Sign-in and accounts built, in sample mode (stage D)
- **The sample store** (`lib/data/store.ts`): one per server process, on `globalThis`, seeded with five sample accounts (student, approved seller, seller waiting for approval, mentor and admin) and wiped when the server restarts. Every read and write goes through `lib/data` functions shaped like Supabase's; stages E to G add their tables to it.
- **Sign in** (`/login`): your Eduvos email and the privacy box, then a 6-digit code (shown on screen in sample mode; 10 minutes, 5 tries, 5 codes an hour), then a first name and campus on a first sign-in. Every step is a plain form posting to a Server Action, so it works without the app's JavaScript. Sample-account buttons sign straight in. `VOSSIE_SAMPLE_MODE=0` turns sign-in off; `VOSSIE_EMAIL_DOMAINS` sets the domains.
- **Sessions:** an httpOnly cookie with a random token, for 30 days; the store keeps only hashes of tokens and codes. The header's icon and the menu's rows come from the server, so there's no readable sign-in cookie.
- **Your account** (`/account`): your details, Edit details, Saved, selling and privacy settings, Sign out. **Privacy settings** (`/account/privacy`): the consent record, Download your data (`vossie-data.json`), and Delete your account, which removes everything at once and signs you out with a toast.
- **Words:** the privacy policy names the three new cookies and describes the preview's accounts; How it works and the save notes say what's true now.
- **Where the build differed from the plan** is in the plan's "Changes made during the build".
```
